# Session 05 — Frontend (P6): plan and decisions, Vue 3 composer, draft and live screens, verification; test-scenarios kickoff prompt (2026-10-21 – 2026-10-22)

## User

<pasted_content>
## Amaç
Smart Campaign Composer take-home'unda P6'yı (Vue 3 frontend) başlat. Bu turda SADECE güncel durumu özetle ve P6'nın detaylı uygulama planını sun. Onayım olmadan kod yazma, dosya değiştirme, paket kurma, branch açma.

## Önce oku (başka hiçbir şey yapmadan)
- docs/CASE.md; docs/PLAN.md (P6 bölümü, P5 Verification log'u, Cut order); DECISIONS.md (özellikle D11, D12, D15, D17, D22, D23, D24, D25 ve A13)
- docs/API.md'nin tamamı. Sözleşme bu; P6 backend'i sadece buradan tüketir.
- DESIGN.md (2–10) ve design/screens/*.png: SADECE görsel referans (D22). Ürün kuralları DECISIONS.md'den gelir. DESIGN.md'nin metin kuralları bağlayıcı değil; UI dili İngilizce (A13).
- frontend/ (src/, package.json, vite.config.ts, vitest.config.ts, nginx.conf, Dockerfile), Makefile (test-frontend), docker-compose.test.yml (frontend-test)
- `git log --oneline -15`

## Bağlam (carry forward)
- P0–P5 tamam, PR #7 (P5) main'e merge edildi. Teslim 2026-10-26; takvim ~2 gün önde. Frontend puanın %20'si.
- Mevcut frontend P1 iskeleti: Vue 3.5, Vite 8, TS 6, vue-router 5, Vitest 4 (3 test). Bir StatusView ve src/api/client.ts var. nginx statik build'i sunuyor ve /api'yi tamponsuz proxy'liyor. UI kütüphanesi, chart, Pinia ve font paketi yok.
- Backend sözleşmesi sabit. P6 backend'e dokunmaz; bir boşluk bulursan dur ve sor.
- SSE kuralları (API.md):
  - Draft stream: `draft.delta.value` alanın o ana kadarki tam metnidir, append etme, replace et.
  - Dashboard: `delivery.snapshot` her seferinde tam durumu taşır; `per_second` serisini her snapshot'ta değiştir. `delivery.done` gelince `es.close()`.
  - Api yeniden başlarken nginx 502 döner ve EventSource kalıcı olarak CLOSED olur. Yeniden bağlanmayı istemci kendisi, backoff ile yapar ("Reconnecting…", son snapshot ekranda kalır).
  - Grafik penceresi 1–2 s geride; bitmiş kampanyada son 30 saniyeye sabitlenir ve `rate` 0 olur. "now / 3s" yaşı snapshot'ın `at` alanından hesaplanır.
- Sayaçlar backend'le aynı sayıyı göstermeli (D15, D22):
  - Limit, `longest_values` ile hesaplanan worst-case uzunluğa göre uygulanır; ham template'e göre değil.
  - Go rune (code point) sayar. JS'te `str.length` UTF-16 sayar: `[...str].length` kullan.
  - Değişkenler: `{{first_name}}`, `{{last_name}}`, `{{city}}` (tasarımda sadece first_name var; D22 geçerli). Emoji yasak (kod `emoji`).
  - Düzenlenen içerik `PUT …/messages/{channel}` ile backend'de yeniden doğrulanır; 422 report'u alanın yanında gösterilir.
- Kapsam dışı (D22): search, notifications, settings, profile, Audiences/Templates sayfaları.
- Canlı doğrulama tuzakları:
  - `.env`'de gerçek key var ve `LLM_PROVIDER=auto`. Stack'i `LLM_PROVIDER=mock docker compose up -d --build --wait` ile kaldır; kampanya açmadan önce `/api/config`'in mock dediğini doğrula.
  - Tek servis için `docker compose stop/start <svc>` kullan.
  - `.env` değerini ASLA yazdırma; `.env.example`'daki key satırı boş kalmalı. P6 ücretli LLM çağrısı gerektirmez.

## P6'da mutlaka ele alınacaklar (ayrıntı PLAN.md P6, D22, DESIGN.md)
- **Composer:** brief, SMS/Email chip'leri, tone, `GET /api/briefs/examples` kütüphanesi, Generate, `/api/config`'ten "Demo mode" rozeti.
- **Draft ekranı:**
  - Audience kartı: koşul chip'leri, kişi sayısı, kanal başına reachable/excluded ve `channel_messages`, unsupported criteria uyarısı, sıfır kitle durumu.
  - Mesaj kartları: writing, validating, fixing n/3, valid, needs_manual_edit; SMS email'den önce valid olabilir.
- **Review & edit:** canlı sayaçlar, değişken ekleme chip'leri, örnek kullanıcı ve worst-case önizleme, alan başına hata.
- **Confirm send modal:** final sayılar ve "This can't be undone". Approve idempotent.
- **Dashboard (08 — Live):**
  - Genel ilerleme ("143 of 232 done"), kanal kartları (Sent/Sending/Queued/Retrying/Failed, rate ve 100/s gauge).
  - 30 s hız grafiği ve kesikli 100/s çizgisi.
  - Recent issues metni UI'da üretilir (`kind`/`outcome`/`retry_in_ms`, D25 K4).
  - Completed ve Reconnecting durumları.
- **Campaign list:** Cut order'da ilk kesilecek UI parçası; planda nereye koyduğunu belirt.
- Tema token'ları tek dosyada; fontlar npm ile self-host. Erişilebilirlik DESIGN.md §8.

## Done kriterleri
- Mock LLM ile tüm akış tarayıcıda çalışır: brief → draft → edit → approve → canlı dashboard → completed.
- Limit aşan bir edit backend tarafından reddedilir ve UI'da görünür.
- Dashboard canlı akışı izler; `docker compose stop api` / `start api` sırasında Reconnecting'e geçer ve veriyi sıfırlamadan devam eder.
- Sayaç değerleri backend report'unun `worst_case_length` değerine eşit (test ile).
- Draft stream ve snapshot işleyen mantık için Vitest testleri, ayrıca birkaç bileşen testi. `make test` yeşil.
- 1440px ve 1024px düzenleri, klavye/focus ve reduced motion kontrol edildi.

## Bu turun çıktısı (Türkçe, bu sırayla)
1. Güncel durum özeti, en fazla 8 madde.
2. P6 adımları tablosu: her adımda ne yapılacağı, doğrulama kanıtı (Vitest, mantık için mutation check, uygulama içi tarayıcıda 1440/1024 ekran görüntüsü, canlı stack) ve tahmini süre.
3. Açık kararlar: her biri için 2–3 seçenek, trade-off'lar ve önerin. En azından:
   - UI kütüphanesi mi, kendi küçük bileşenlerimiz mi (D17'nin P6'ya bıraktığı karar);
   - grafik: kendi SVG'miz mi, kütüphane mi;
   - durum yönetimi: Pinia mı, composable'lar mı;
   - SSE istemcisi ve yeniden bağlanma stratejisi (backoff, 502, done);
   - sayaç hesabı: istemcide worst-case hesabı mı, backend report'unu beklemek mi (ya da ikisi birden);
   - font seçimi ve self-host yolu;
   - test yaklaşımı (Vitest + test-utils yeterli mi; e2e gerekirse yeni bağımlılık olur).

Sonra DUR ve onayımı bekle.

## Çalışma kuralları
- Benimle Türkçe konuş; kod, commit mesajları ve dokümanlar İngilizce olsun.
- Onaydan sonra: main'den `feat/p6-frontend` branch'i aç, her adım ayrı commit, her adımın sonunda doğrulama, en sonda PR.
- docs/PLAN.md verification log'u güncellenecek. DECISIONS.md'de D17 kapatılacak ve "Frontend as built" için yeni bir D26 eklenecek.
- Mutation check prosedürü (P5 ile aynı):
  - Değişiklik tam bir kez uygulanmış olmalı (diff'i göster).
  - Mutant derlenmeli (`vue-tsc` ve `vite build`); derlenmiyorsa sonuç geçersizdir.
  - Testler filtrelenmeden çalışmalı ve en az bir test adıyla kırılmalı.
  - Geri al, yedekle `cmp` ile karşılaştır, `git status`'un temiz olduğunu doğrula.
  - Kurulumu `&&` zincirine güvenmeden kontrol eden bir script kullan. Test çıktısını build hatalarını veya `FAIL` satırlarını gizleyecek şekilde filtreleme.
- Zamanlamaya dayalı testleri birden çok kez çalıştırıp kararsız olmadıklarını göster.
- Sadece istenen değişiklikleri yap. Ekstra özellik, soyutlama ya da dosya ekleme.

## Dur ve sor
- Herhangi bir yeni npm bağımlılığından önce (UI kütüphanesi, grafik, Pinia, font paketleri, Playwright dahil): paket, sürüm, yayın tarihi (en az iki haftalık) ve nedeni göster.
- Backend kodu, API sözleşmesi, migration veya compose değişikliğinden önce.
- Dosya silmeden önce.
- PLAN.md, DECISIONS.md veya API.md ile çelişen bir şey bulursan.
- Herhangi bir ücretli API çağrısından önce (P6'da gerekmemeli).

1	# API contract
2	
3	> REST and SSE contract of the api service, as built in P3, P4 and P5. It is the input for the frontend (P6).
4	> Rationale: [`DECISIONS.md`](../DECISIONS.md). Examples below are real responses from the mock LLM.
5	
6	## Conventions
7	
8	- Base URL: `http://localhost:8080/api` through the frontend's nginx (the api itself listens on `:8081`).
9	  nginx does not buffer `/api/`, so server-sent events arrive as they are written.
10	- Request and response bodies are JSON (UTF-8). Request bodies are decoded strictly: an unknown key is a `422`.
11	- Lengths are Unicode characters (runes), never bytes.
12	- Errors:
13	
14	  | Status | Body | When |
15	  |---|---|---|
16	  | `404` | `{"error": "campaign not found"}` | unknown id or channel |
17	  | `409` | `{"error": "..."}` | content is locked, drafts are already being written, or the campaign is not ready to approve |
18	  | `413` | `{"error": "request body is too large"}` | body over 64 KB |
19	  | `422` | `{"error": "...", "details": ["...", ...]}` | invalid input; `details` lists every problem |
20	  | `422` | `{"error": "invalid content", "report": {...}}` | an edited message failed validation (see [Report](#report)) |
21	  | `422` | `{"error": "...", "channel": "sms", "report": {...}}` | at approval, a message no longer fits the audience |
22	  | `500` | `{"error": "internal error"}` | details are logged, never returned |
23	
24	## LLM mode
25	
26	### `GET /api/config`
27	
28	Which LLM answers, for the UI's "Demo mode" badge (D12).
29	
30	```json
31	{"llm": {"mode": "mock", "model": null}}
32	{"llm": {"mode": "anthropic", "model": "claude-sonnet-5-5"}}
33	```
34	
35	Chosen at startup by `LLM_PROVIDER` (`auto` = Claude when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set, mock otherwise).
36	
37	## Brief library
38	
39	### `GET /api/briefs/examples`
40	
41	Ready-made briefs. With the mock LLM each one runs a scripted, deterministic scenario; with Claude they are ordinary briefs.
42	
43	```json
44	{
45	  "examples": [
46	    {
47	      "id": "cart-reminder-istanbul",
48	      "title": "Cart reminder — İstanbul",
49	      "brief": "Son 30 günde sepetine ürün ekleyip alışveriş yapmayan İstanbul'daki kullanıcılara %30 indirim hatırlatması gönder. Samimi ama kısa olsun.",
50	      "channels": ["sms", "email"],
51	      "tone": "friendly"
52	    }
53	  ]
54	}
55	```
56	
57	Five examples: cart reminder (İstanbul), win-back 90 days, new season (SMS only, with an unsupported criterion), weekend sale (email only, all 
… [26940 more characters truncated]

1	# Composer — frontend design specification
2	
3	Smart Campaign Composer lets a marketer write a brief, review the resulting audience and SMS/email drafts, edit and approve the content, then follow sending progress.
4	
5	**Authority:** written requirements determine values, copy and counts. The original reference and `01-composer.png`, `02-draft-generating.png`, `08-live-sending.png` inform layout, composition and component shapes only. Never transcribe image text or numbers. This document is the frontend source of truth. Scope: light theme, desktop first at 1440px, usable at 1024px.
6	
7	`Assumption:` identifies implementation details missing from the written requirements. It does not authorize additional product features.
8	
9	## 1. Visual theme & atmosphere
10	
11	Light, airy glassmorphism: cool background, broad aqua/sky-blue glows and a low peach glow. Translucent white surfaces have soft depth, fine white borders and an upper highlight. Navy anchors navigation; coral-to-pink highlights primary actions. Use generous whitespace, quiet outline icons and clear text. Blobs are atmospheric, never discrete illustrations.
12	
13	## 2. Color tokens
14	
15	These base colors come from the written design system. Alias values reference other tokens rather than introducing colors.
16

// Thin fetch wrapper: JSON in/out, and non-2xx responses become ApiError with
// the server's {"error": "..."} message when there is one.

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly status: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'ApiError'
  }
}

export async function getJSON<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(path, {
    ...init,
    headers: { Accept: 'application/json', ...init?.headers },
  })
  if (!res.ok) {
    let message = `Request failed with status ${res.status}`
    try {
      const body = (await res.json()) as { error?: unknown }
      if (typeof body.error === 'string') message = body.error
    } catch {
      // Body was not JSON (e.g. a proxy error page); keep the generic message.
    }
    throw new ApiError(res.status, message)
  }
  return (await res.json()) as T
}

// Package content holds the rules for campaign messages: channels and their
// length limits, personalization variables, validation and rendering
// (DECISIONS.md D15). The same rules apply to Claude's drafts and to the
// marketer's edits.
package content
// Channel is a delivery channel.
type Channel string
const (
	SMS   Channel = "sms"
	Email Channel = "email"
)
// Channels lists every channel in a stable order.
var Channels = []Channel{SMS, Email}
// Valid reports whether c is a known channel.
func (c Channel) Valid() bool {
	return c == SMS || c == Email
}
// Limits from the case, counted in Unicode characters (runes), not bytes.
const (
	MaxSMSRunes     = 160
	MaxSubjectRunes = 80
)
// Variable is a personalization variable, written {{name}} in a template.
type Variable string
const (
	FirstName Variable = "first_name"
	LastName  Variable = "last_name"
	City      Variable = "city"
)
// Variables is the whitelist, in a stable order.
var Variables = []Variable{FirstName, LastName, City}
// Placeholder is the variable as written in a template, e.g. "{{first_name}}".
func (v Variable) Placeholder() string {
	return "{{" + string(v) + "}}"
}
// Values maps each variable to one value: a recipient's data, the fictional
// sample user, or the longest values in an audience.
type Values map[Variable]string
// Message is one channel's content. SMS uses Text; email uses Subject and Body.
type Message struct {
	Text    string `json:"text,omitempty"`
	Subject string `json:"subject,omitempty"`
	Body    string `json:"body,omitempty"`
}

package content

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

// Fallbacks replace an empty value when a message is rendered. The users
// table forbids empty names and cities, so they are a defensive default;
// the worst-case length still accounts for them.
var Fallbacks = Values{
	FirstName: "Müşterimiz",
	LastName:  "",
	City:      "",
}

// token is one piece of a parsed template: literal text or a variable.
type token struct {
	text string   // literal text (when variable is empty)
	v    Variable // the variable, for {{name}} tokens
}

// parse splits a template into literal text and whitelisted variables. It
// reports every malformed or unknown variable instead of stopping at the
// first, so one repair round can fix them all.
func parse(field, template string) ([]token, Errors) {
	var toks []token
	var errs Errors
	rest := template
	for rest != "" {
		open := strings.Index(rest, "{{")
		closeBeforeOpen := strings.Index(rest, "}}")
		if closeBeforeOpen >= 0 && (open < 0 || closeBeforeOpen < open) {
			errs = append(errs, FieldError{Field: field, Code: CodeMalformedVariable,
				Message: fmt.Sprintf(`%s has "}}" without a matching "{{"; write variables exactly as %s`, field, allowedList())})
			toks = append(toks, token{text: rest[:closeBeforeOpen+2]})
			rest = rest[closeBeforeOpen+2:]
			continue
		}
		if open < 0 {
			toks = append(toks, token{text: rest})
			break
		}
		if open > 0 {
			toks = append(toks, token{text: rest[:open]})
		}
		end := strings.Index(rest[open+2:], "}}")
		if end < 0 {
			errs = append(errs, FieldError{Field: field, Code: CodeMalformedVariable,
				Message: fmt.Sprintf(`%s has "{{" that is never closed; write variables exactly as %s`, field, allowedList())})
			toks = append(toks, token{text: rest[open:]})
			break
		}
		name := rest[open+2 : open+2+end]
		raw := rest[open : open+2+end+2]
		rest = rest[open+2+end+2:]

		switch v, ok := lookupVariable(name); {
		case ok:
			toks = append(toks, token{v: v})
		case isVariableLike(strings.TrimSpace(name)):
			if tv, ok := lookupVariable(strings.TrimSpace(name)); ok {
				errs = append(errs, FieldError{Field: field, Code: CodeMalformedVariable,
					Message: fmt.Sprintf(`%s has %q; write it exactly as %s, without spaces`, field, raw, tv.Placeholder())})
			} else {
				errs = append(errs, FieldError{Field: field, Code: CodeUnknownVariable,
					Message: fmt.Sprintf("%s uses unknown variable %s; allowed: %s", field, raw, allowedList())})
			}
			toks = append(toks, token{text: raw})
		defaul

```
// edit is rejected and the stored message is left as it was.
func (s *Service) EditMessage(ctx context.Context, id int64, ch content.Channel, m content.Message) (MessageView, Status, error) {
	c, err := s.Repo.Get(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return MessageView{}, "", err
	}
	if !slices.Contains(c.Channels, ch) {
		return MessageView{}, "", ErrNotFound
	}
	if !c.Status.Editable() {
		return MessageView{}, c.Status, ErrLocked
	}
	_, longest, err := s.audience(ctx, c)
	if err != nil {
		return MessageView{}, "", err
	}
	report := content.Validate(ch, m, longest[ch])
	if !report.Valid {
		return MessageView{}, c.Status, &ContentError{Report: report}
	}
	stored := StoredMessage{State: llm.StateValid, Message: m, Source: SourceEdited, Problems: []string{}}
	if prev, ok := c.Messages[ch]; ok {
		stored.Attempts = prev.Attempts
	}
	status, err := s.Repo.SaveMessage(ctx, id, ch, stored)
	if err != nil {
		return MessageView{}, status, err
	}
	stored.UpdatedAt = s.Now().UTC()
	return messageView(ch, stored, longest[ch]), status, nil
}

func toStored(r llm.ChannelResult) StoredMessage {
	m := StoredMessage{State: r.State, Source: SourceGenerated, Attempts: r.Attempts, Problems: r.Problems}
	if r.Message != nil {
		m.Message = *r.Message
	}
	if m.Problems == nil {
		m.Problems = []string{}
	}
	return m
}

// storedEvent replays a stored message as the final draft.state event.
func storedEvent(ch content.Channel, m StoredMessage, longest content.Values) llm.Event {
	r := llm.ChannelResult{Channel: ch, State: m.State, Attempts: m.Attempts, Problems: m.Problems}
	if hasContent(ch, m.Message) {
		msg := m.Message
		report := content.Validate(ch, msg, longest)
		r.Message, r.Report = &msg, &report
	}
	return llm.Event{Type: llm.EventState, Channel: ch, Attempt: m.Attempts, State: m.State,
		MaxAttempts: llm.MaxAttempts, Result: &r}
}

func hasContent(ch content.Channel, m content.Message) bool {
	if ch == content.Email {
		return m.Subject != "" || m.Body != ""
	}
	return m.Text != ""
}

	ID                  int64                            `json:"id"`
	Name                string                           `json:"name"`
	Status              Status                           `json:"status"`
	Brief               string

  node_type: memory

- Plan first; never write code before the user approves.
- Work phase by phase; test and verify at the end of each phase.
- For ambiguous decisions, present 2-3 options with trade-offs plus a recommendation; the user decides.
- Talk to the user in Turkish; code, commit messages and documents in English.
- Core flow first; bonus items only after the core is solid.

**Why:** sessions are exported to `ai-sessions/` and graded on problem approach and how AI output was verified, not on volume of AI use.
**How to apply:** every phase ends with explicit verification evidence (tests, curl output, clean-clone run). Related: [[user-background]], [[campaign-composer-case]].

==> live-stack-gotchas.md <==
  node_type: memory
  type: project
  originSessionId: a3185aeb-3baf-4fc2-bbe1-923e2ebdd8b7
  modified: 2026-10-19T21:38:30.899Z
---

Learned during P4 live checks (2026-10-20):

- `.env` holds a real key and `LLM_PROVIDER=auto`, so a plain `docker compose up` runs the api against Claude (paid). For live checks start with `LLM_PROVIDER=mock docker compose up -d --build --wait` and confirm `curl localhost:8080/api/config` says mock before creating campaigns.
- `docker compose up -d <svc>` can recreate dependencies (it recreated mock-provider mid-campaign and wiped its in-memory idempotency store/stats). Restart a single service with `docker compose start/stop <svc>`, and compare the mock provider's StartedAt before/after.
- Every container on this machine gets `StopTimeout=1` (not from the compose file), so SIGTERM becomes SIGKILL after 1 s. The worker now has `stop_grace_period: 10s`; the api (graceful HTTP/SSE shutdown) still has the 1 s default — candidate for P7 hardening.
- Postgres in Docker runs ~1.8 ms behind the host clock; don't mix DB `now()` and Go times in one comparison.

**Why:** each of these produced a misleading result once in P4.
**How to apply:** before any live verification in later phases (P5 SSE, P7 clean-clone run). Related: [[env-example-key-check]], [[campaign-composer-case]].

Hiçbir dosyayı değiştirmedim, paket kurmadım, branch açmadım. Sadece dosyaları okudum ve npm registry'den sürüm bilgisi sorguladım.

## 1. Güncel durum

- **Fazlar:** P0–P5 tamam. `main` temiz, son commit `a595591` (PR #7 merge). Takvim P6'yı Cuma–Cumartesi'ye koymuştu; bugün Çarşamba, yani yaklaşık 2 gün öndeyiz. Teslim 26 Ekim Pazartesi.
- **Backend sözleşmesi:** API.md P6'nın ihtiyacını karşılıyor: config, örnek brief'ler, kampanya oluşturma/okuma/listeleme, draft SSE, `PUT …/messages`, idempotent approve, dashboard SSE (snapshot ve recent issues). Tek istisna son maddedeki boşluk.
- **Frontend bugün:** P1 iskeleti. Vue 3.5.42, vue-router 5.3.1, Vite 8.2, TS 6.0, Vitest 4.1, test-utils 2.5, jsdom 30. Tek ekran `StatusView` var (3 test).
  - `api/client.ts` sadece GET yapıyor ve 422'nin `details`/`report` gövdesini atıyor.
  - `base.css` dark mode içeriyor; tasarım sadece light.
- **Altyapı:** nginx `/api/` isteklerini tamponsuz proxy'liyor, read timeout 1 saat, SPA fallback var. `make test-frontend` sadece `vitest --run` çalıştırıyor. `vue-tsc` yalnızca image build'inde koşuyor, yani test dosyalarındaki tip hataları `make test`'i kırmıyor.
- **Tasarım görselleri:** Sadece 3 ekran var (01, 02, 08). Review, Confirm, Completed, Reconnecting ve sıfır kitle yalnızca DESIGN.md §6'da metin olarak tarif ediliyor.
- **Mock katalog:** 5 örnek brief kütüphaneden geliyor. 7 hata senaryosu, `[test] …` brief'i composer'a yazılınca tetikleniyor. Böylece fixing, needs_manual_edit ve boş mesaj durumlarını key olmadan tarayıcıda gösterebiliriz.
- **Sayaç kuralı (backend kodundan okudum: `content/template.go`, `validate.go`):** `report.longest_values` fallback'i zaten uygulanmış değerler (ör. `Müşterimiz`). Hatalı veya bilinmeyen değişkenler (`{{ first_name }}`, `{{name}}`, kapanmamış `{{`) düz metin olarak sayılıyor. İstemci portu bunu birebir kopyalamalı.
- **Bulduğum boşluk:** `longest_values` sadece bir mesajın `report`'unda geliyor. İki durumda istemci backend'in uyguladığı sayıyı hesaplayamıyor:
  - Mesaj hiç yokken. `fault-refusal` SMS'inde ve `fault-timeout` email'inde hem `message` hem `report` `null` geliyor.
  - Stream sırasında, kanalın ilk `result`'ı gelmeden önce.

  Karar K5'te.

## 2. P6 adımları

Her adım ayrı bir commit olacak.

Bu tarayıcı `prefers-reduced-motion`'ı taklit edemiyor. Bu yüzden hesaplanmış stilleri kontrol ederim, ayrıca senin macOS'ta "Reduce motion"ı açıp bakman gerekir. | 1,5 sa |

**Toplam:** S3b dahil yaklaşık 18,5 saat. Plandaki 10–12 saatin üstünde.
- **Takvim:** Perşembe S1–S3b, Cuma S4–S5, Cumartesi S6–S9. P7 Pazar, Pazartesi tampon.
- **Kesme sırası:** önce S7 (Cut order 3), sonra dashboard cilası (Cut order 7). Kampanya listesi S7'de ve tek başına duruyor. Kesilirse composer'daki "Recent campaigns" bölümü de gider.

**Mutantlar (S2, mantık için):**
- delta metne append ediliyor
- fixing'de metin siliniyor
- `s.length` kullanılıyor. Fixture'da `𝟑` gibi BMP dışı bir karakter şart; Türkçe harfler BMP içinde olduğu için bu mutantı yakalamaz.
- worst case yerine ham template uzunluğu sayılıyor
- `{{ first_name }}` değerle dolduruluyor
- near-limit eşiği `>` yazılmış (doğrusu `>=`; 144/160 sınırı)
- `per_second` her snapshot'ta değiştirilmiyor, sona ekleniyor
- `delivery.done` geldiğinde stream kapatılmıyor
- CLOSED durumunda yeniden açılmıyor (502 vakası)
- başarılı bir event'ten sonra backoff sıfırlanmıyor
- issue yaşı `at` yerine `Date.now()` ile hesaplanıyor
- `failed` olan issue "retrying" diye gösteriliyor

**Canlı hazırlık:**
- Stack `LLM_PROVIDER=mock docker compose up -d --build --wait` ile kalkar; kampanya açmadan önce `/api/config` mock dediği doğrulanır.
- Geliştirirken `npm run dev` kullanırım (Vite proxy'si → :8081). Son kontroller :8080'deki nginx image'ında yapılır.

```
src/styles/      theme.css · base.css
src/api/         client.ts · types.ts · campaigns.ts
src/logic/       content.ts · draft.ts · snapshot.ts · stream.ts   (pure TS, most of the tests)
src/composables/ useConfig.ts · useDraftStream.ts · useDeliveryStream.ts
src/components/  ui/ · layout/ · campaign/
src/views/       ComposerView · CampaignDraftView · CampaignLiveView · CampaignsView
```

## 3. Açık kararlar

**K1 — UI kütüphanesi (D17)**
- **a) Kendi küçük bileşenlerimiz + native öğeler** (`<dialog>`, `<select>`, `aria-pressed`): bağımlılık yok, cam stil token'lar ve DESIGN §10'daki yaklaşık 15 bileşenle kuruluyor, her satırı review'da savunabilirsin. Maliyeti: erişilebilirliği biz yazıp test ediyoruz.
- **b) Naive UI 2.45.3** (2026-08-27): hazır bileşenler, ama cam görünüm için çoğunu ezmek gerekir; CSS-in-JS ve daha büyük bundle.
- **c) Reka UI 2.11.0** (2026-10-05, headless) ve kendi stillerimiz: erişilebilirlik hazır gelir, ama zor iki parçayı (modal, liste) native `<dialog>` zaten çözüyor.
- İkonlar paket olmadan: Lucide'dan yaklaşık 15 outline SVG, inline, ISC lisans notuyla.
- **Önerim: a.**

**K2 — Grafik**
- **a) Kendi SVG'miz** (yaklaşık 100 satır): 2×30 nokta, sabit 0–100 ekseni, kesikli limit çizgisi ve metin özeti. Reconnecting'de dondurmak ve reduced motion'a uymak kolay; jsdom'da test edilebilir. Eğri monotone cubic olacak: normal spline 100'ün üstüne taşıp grafiğin kanıtladığı şeyi, yani limitin aşılmadığını, yanlış gösterebilir. Bunu bir testle kanıtlarız.
- **b) Chart.js 4.5.1 + vue-chartjs 5.3.4:** canvas tabanlı; jsdom'da çizilmez, erişilebilirlik ayrıca yapılmalı, gzip'li yaklaşık 70 KB.
- **c) ECharts 6.1.0 + vue-echarts 8.3.1:** bu iş için fazla ağır.
- **Önerim: a.**

**K3 — Durum yönetimi**
- **a) Composable'lar + saf `logic/` modülleri:** ekranlar arasında paylaşılan tek veri `/api/config`. Kampanya her ekranda id ile yeniden okunur; sayfa yenilemesi de böylece çalışır.
- **b) Pinia 4.0.3** (2026-08-12; 4.1.0 henüz 7 günlük): devtools ve rotalar arası önbellek verir, bu boyutta gereksiz bir katman.
- **Önerim: a.**

**K4 — SSE istemcisi ve yeniden bağlanma**
- **a) `EventSource` + kendi sarmalayıcımız:**
  - `*.done` gelince `close()`.
  - `onerror` ve `readyState` CONNECTING ise tarayıcı kendisi yeniden deniyor (sunucunun `retry: 3000`'i); ekranda "Reconnecting…".
  - `onerror` ve CLOSED ise (502/404/409; EventSource durum kodunu göstermez) `GET /api/campaigns/{id}` ile sınıflandırırız:
    - 404 → hata ekranı
    - draft/ready → draft ekranına
    - ağ hatası/5xx → backoff ile yeniden aç: 1→2→4→8→10 sn tavan, ±%20 jitter, ilk event'te sıfırlanır; banner'da "Try now"
  - Dashboard'da watchdog: 5 sn hiç event gelmezse stream kapatılıp yeniden açılır.
  - Son snapshot ekranda kalır; yeni gelen ilk snapshot onu tamamen değiştirir.
- **b) `fetch` + kendi SSE ayrıştırıcımız:** durum kodlarını doğrudan görürüz, ama ayrıştırıcıyı biz yazıp savunuruz ve sözleşme EventSource'a göre yazıldı.
- **c) Sadece `EventSource`:** 502'den sonra kalıcı olarak CLOSED kalır, done kriteri karşılanmaz.
- **Önerim: a.** Draft stream de aynı sarmalayıcıyı kullanır, watchdog olmadan.

**K5 — Sayaç hesabı (+ backend'e dokunan bir soru)**
- **a) İstemcide worst case** (Go parse/render portu + `longest_values`); kaydederken backend report'u yetkili kaynak.
- **b) Sadece backend report'u:** canlı sayaç olmaz.
- **c) Ham template uzunluğu:** D15 ile çelişir.
- **Önerim: a.** Kanıt olarak kaydedilmiş fixture testi:
  - Küçük bir script (`frontend/scripts/record-worst-case.mjs`, yeni dosya; P3'teki `-tags live` kaydına benziyor) canlı api'ye yaklaşık 200 template'i `PUT` eder. 422 dönenler hiçbir şey yazmaz.
  - Sonuçları JSON olarak commit'leriz. Vitest, istemci hesabının her biriyle aynı çıktığını kontrol eder; `make test` stack istemez.
- **Boşluk için:**
  - **i) Backend'e additive alan:** campaign view'a kanal başına `longest_values` eklenir (S3b). Mevcut alanlar değişmez. Stream sırasında ve mesajı olmayan kanalda da sayaç backend'in sayısını gösterir.
  - **ii) Backend'e dokunmadan:** report gelene kadar ham uzunluk "Not checked yet" etiketiyle gösterilir. D15'teki "ekran validasyonla çelişmez" sözü o anlarda tutmaz.
  - **iii) Boş metinle `PUT` atıp 422 report'undan değerleri almak:** çalışır ama bir hile.
  - **Önerim: i.**

**K6 — Font**
- **a) `@fontsource-variable/inter`, `/urbanist`, `/jetbrains-mono`, üçü de 5.3.0** (2026-07-19, OFL): değişken font; `unicode-range` sayesinde tarayıcı sadece gereken subset'leri indirir. ş/ğ/ı/İ latin-ext'te; kurulumda dosya listesiyle doğrularım.
- **b) `@fontsource/*` statik 5.3.0:** ağırlıklar tek tek import edilir, daha çok dosya.
- **c) woff2 dosyalarını elle `public/` altına koymak:** "npm ile self-host" kararına uymaz.
- **Önerim: a.**

**K7 — Test yaklaşımı**
- **a) Mevcut Vitest + test-utils + jsdom:** ağırlık saf mantığın birim testlerinde, birkaç bileşen testi, fixture testi ve mutasyon kontrolleri. Uçtan uca akış uygulama içi tarayıcıda canlı stack'le, ekran görüntüleriyle log'a yazılır. Yeni bağımlılık yok.
- **b) + Playwright 1.64.0** (2026-10-07): `make test`'e tam stack ve yaklaşık 1 GB'lık tarayıcı image'ı ekler; kararsızlık riski var.
- **c) Vitest browser mode:** o da Playwright ister.
- **Önerim: a.** P7'deki smoke script API akışını zaten kapsıyor.

**Ek kararlar:**
- **K8 — Editör:**
  - a) Textarea'da ham `{{first_name}}`; pill'ler sadece okuma modunda ve önizlemede görünür. Sağlam: Türkçe klavye, yapıştırma ve undo sorunsuz.
  - b) `contenteditable` içinde atomik pill'ler: tasarıma birebir ama imleç ve IME hataları çıkar, test etmesi zor.
  - c) Textarea + arkada vurgulama katmanı: hizalama kırılgan.
  - Önerim a; DESIGN'dan bu sapma D26'ya yazılır.
- **K9 — Kapsam dışı öğeler:** Audiences, Templates, arama, zil, ayarlar ve profil hiç gösterilmesin (önerim) mi, yoksa disabled mı görünsün? Karşılama yerel saate göre "Good morning/afternoon/evening" olur, isim olmadan (A10).
- **K10 — Silme onayı:** P1'den kalan `StatusView.vue`, testi ve `api/stats.ts` silinsin mi? Backend'deki `/api/stats/users` kalır. Önerim: evet.
- **K11 — `make test`'te tip kontrolü:** `frontend/Dockerfile`'ın test stage'i `vue-tsc --build` da çalıştırsın mı? Compose değişmez, yaklaşık 10 sn ekler. Önerim: evet.

Onayını bekliyorum. Özellikle şunlara açık bir "evet" lazım:
- K5-i: backend eklemesi
- K6: 3 font paketi
- K5'teki kayıt script'i
- K10: silme
- K11: Dockerfile değişikliği

Onaydan sonra `feat/p6-frontend` branch'ini açıp S1'den başlarım.

)

// Terminal reports whether no more events follow for the channel.
func (s State) Terminal() bool { return s == StateValid || s == StateNeedsManualEdit }

// Event types sent to the client (docs/API.md).
const (
	EventState = "draft.state"
	EventDelta = "draft.delta"
)

// Event is one step of a channel's draft.
type Event struct {
	Type    string          `json:"-"`
	Channel content.Channel `json:"channel"`
	Attempt int             `json:"attempt"`

	// draft.state
	State       State    `json:"state,omitempty"`
	MaxAttempts int      `json:"max_attempts,omitempty"`
	Problems    []string `json:"problems,omitempty"` // what the fixing attempt corrects
	// Result is set when State is terminal.
	Result *ChannelResult `json:"result,omitempty"`

	// draft.delta: the current value of one field, decoded so far. Each
	// delta replaces the previous value of that field.
	Field string `json:"field,omitempty"`
	Value string `json:"value,omitempty"`
}

// ChannelResult is a channel's final draft.
type ChannelResult struct {
	Channel  content.Channel `json:"channel"`
	State    State           `json:"state"` // valid or needs_manual_edit
	Attempts int             `json:"attempts"`
	// Message is the last parsed draft; nil if the model never produced one.
	Message *content.Message `json:"message"`
	// Report validates Message; nil when Message is nil.
	Report *content.Report `json:"report"`
	// Problems explain needs_manual_edit; empty when valid.
	Problems []string `json:"problems"`
	Usage    Usage    `json:"usage"`
}

// DraftInput is everything the copywriter is told.
type DraftInput struct {
	Brief        string
	Tone         Tone

var(--focus-width) solid var(--color-focus);\n  outline-offset: var(--focus-offset);\n}\n\n/* Glass surface (DESIGN.md section 4). */\n.glass {\n  background: var(--color-glass);\n  border: var(--border-thin) solid var(--color-glass-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-card);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card), var(--shadow-highlight);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-glass));\n  "content": "// Shapes of the api's JSON, as documented in docs/API.md. Field names stay in\n// the api's snake_case so a response can be compared with the docs line by line.\n\nexport type Channel = 'sms' | 'email'\nexport const CHANNELS: readonly Channel[] = ['sms', 'email']\n\nexport type Tone = 'friendly' | 'professional' | 'playful' | 'urgent'\nexport const TONES: readonly Tone[] = ['friendly', 'professional', 'playful', 'urgent']\n\nexport type CampaignStatus = 'draft' | 'ready' | 'sending' | 'completed' | 'failed'\nexport type MessageStatus = 'queued' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'retrying' | 'failed'\nexport type DraftState = 'writing' | 'validating' | 'fixing' | 'valid' | 'needs_manual_edit'\n\nexport type Variable = 'first_name' | 'last_name' | 'city'\nexport const VARIABLES: readonly Variable[] = ['first_name', 'last_name', 'city']\n\n/** One value per variable (a recipient, the sample user, or an audience's longest values). */\nexport type Values = Partial<Record<Variable, string>>\n\nexport interface AppConfig {\n  llm: { mode: 'mock' | 'anthropic'; model: string | null }\n}\n\nexport interface BriefExample {\n  id: string\n  title: string\n  brief: string\n  channels: Channel[]\n  tone: Tone\n}\n\nexport interface Reach {\n  total: number\n  sms_reachable: number\n  email_reachable: number\n  sms_excluded: number\n  email_excluded: number\n  channel_messages: number\n}\n\nexport interface Audience extends Reach {\n  description: { summary: string; lines: string[] }\n  /** Per selected channel: the values the worst-case length is computed with (D15). */\n  longest_values: Partial<Record<Channel, Values>>\n}\n\nexport type Field = 'text' | 'subject' | 'body'\n\n/** SMS uses text; email uses subject and body. Templates keep their {{variables}}. */\nexport interface MessageContent {\n  text?: string\n  subject?: string\n  body?: string\n}\n\nexport interface FieldError {\n  field: Field\n  code: 'empty' | 'too_long' | 'unknown_variable' | 'malformed_variable' | 'multiline' | 'emoji'\n  message: string\n}\n\nexport interface FieldLength {\n  field: Field\n  template_length: number\n  worst_case_length: number\n  limit: number\n}\n\nexport interface Report {\n  valid: boolean\n  errors: FieldError[]\n  lengths: FieldLength[]\n  longest_values: Values\n  preview: { sample_user: Values; sample: MessageContent; worst_case: MessageContent }\n}\n\nexport interface MessageView {\n  state: 'valid' | 'needs_manual_edit'\n  source: 'generated' | 'edited'\n  attempts: number\n  message: MessageContent | null\n  report: Report | null\n  problems: string[]\n  updated_at: string\n}\n\nexport interface CampaignView {\n  id: number\n  name: string\n  status: CampaignStatus\n  brief: string\n  tone: Tone\n  channels: Channel[]\n  audience: Audience\n  unsupported_criteria: string[]\n  as_of: string\n  llm: AppConfig['llm']\n  messages: Partial<Record<Channel, MessageView | null>>\n  approved_at: string | null\n  finished_at: string | null\n  created_at: string\n  updated_at: string\n}\n\nexport interface CampaignSummary {\n  id: number\n  name: string\n  status: CampaignStatus\n  channels: Channel[]\n  audience: Reach\n  created_at: string\n}\n\nexport interface CreateCampaignRequest {\n  brief: string\n  channels: Channel[]\n  tone: Tone\n}\n\nexport interface SaveMessageResponse {\n  message: MessageView\n  status: CampaignStatus\n}\n\nexport interface ApproveResponse {\n  campaign_id: number\n  status: CampaignStatus\n  approved_at: string\n  deliveries: { sms: number; email: number; total: number }\n}\n\n// ---- Draft stream (GET /api/campaigns/{id}/draft/stream) ----\n\nexport interface ChannelResult {\n  channel: Channel\n  state: 'valid' | 'needs_manual_edit'\n  attempts: number\n  message: MessageContent | null\n  report: Report | null\n  problems: string[]\n}\n\nexport interface DraftStateEvent {\n  channel: Channel\n  attempt: number

{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/api/client.ts",
 "content": "// Thin fetch wrapper: JSON in and out. A non-2xx response becomes an ApiError\n// that keeps the server's message and its whole JSON body (a 422 carries\n// `details` or a validation `report`, docs/API.md).\n\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  constructor(\n    /** HTTP status; 0 when the server could not be reached at all. */\n    public readonly status: number,\n    message: string,\n    /** The parsed JSON body of the error response, if it had one. */\n    public readonly body: unknown = null,\n  ) {\n    super(message)\n    this.name = 'ApiError'\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * True when the request never got an answer from the api: the network failed,\n * or nginx answered for an api that is down or restarting (502/503/504).\n */\nexport function isUnreachable(e: unknown): boolean {\n  return e instanceof ApiError && (e.status === 0 || e.status === 502 || e.status === 503 || e.status === 504)\n}\n\nexport async function requestJSON<T>(\n  method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT',\n  path: string,\n  body?: unknown,\n): Promise<T> {\n  let res: Response\n  try {\n    res = await fetch(path, {\n      method,\n      headers:\n        body === undefined\n          ? { Accept: 'application/json' }\n          : { Accept: 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),\n    })\n  } catch {\n    throw new ApiError(0, \"Can't reach the server\")\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    let message = `Request failed with status ${res.status}`\n    let parsed: unknown = null\n    try {\n      parsed = await res.json()\n      const error = (parsed as { error?: unknown }).error\n      if (typeof error === 'string') message = error\n    } catch {\n      // Body was not JSON (e.g. nginx's 502 page); keep the generic message.\n    }\n    throw new ApiError(res.status, message, parsed)\n  }\n  return (await res.json()) as T\n}\n\nexport function getJSON<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {\n  return requestJSON<T>('GET', path)\n}\n"
}

{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/api/campaigns.ts",
 "content": "import { getJSON, requestJSON } from './client'\nimport type {\n  AppConfig,\n  ApproveResponse,\n  BriefExample,\n  CampaignSummary,\n  CampaignView,\n  Channel,\n  CreateCampaignRequest,\n  MessageContent,\n  SaveMessageResponse,\n} from './types'\n\nexport function fetchConfig(): Promise<AppConfig> {\n  return getJSON<AppConfig>('/api/config')\n}\n\nexport async function fetchExamples(): Promise<BriefExample[]> {\n  return (await getJSON<{ examples: BriefExample[] }>('/api/briefs/examples')).examples\n}\n\nexport function createCampaign(req: CreateCampaignRequest): Promise<CampaignView> {\n  return requestJSON<CampaignView>('POST', '/api/campaigns', req)\n}\n\nexport async function fetchCampaigns(): Promise<CampaignSummary[]> {\n  return (await getJSON<{ campaigns: CampaignSummary[] }>('/api/campaigns')).campaigns\n}\n\nexport function fetchCampaign(id: number): Promise<CampaignView> {\n  return getJSON<CampaignView>(`/api/campaigns/${id}`)\n}\n\nexport function saveMessage(\n  id: number,\n  channel: Channel,\n  content: MessageContent,\n): Promise<SaveMessageResponse> {\n  return requestJSON<SaveMessageResponse>('PUT', `/api/campaigns/${id}/messages/${channel}`, content)\n}\n\nexport function approveCampaign(id: number): Promise<ApproveResponse> {\n  return requestJSON<ApproveResponse>('POST', `/api/campaigns/${id}/approve`)\n}\n\nexport function draftStreamURL(id: number): string {\n  return `/api/campaigns/${id}/draft/stream`\n}\n\nexport function eventsURL(id: number): string {\n  return `/api/campaigns/${id}/events`\n}\n"
}

 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/logic/labels.ts",
 "content": "// The UI's wording for the api's codes (docs/API.md tables). One place, so a\n// status never gets two names on two screens (DESIGN.md section 5).\n\nimport type { CampaignStatus, Channel, DraftState, MessageStatus, Tone } from '@/api/types'\n\nexport const CAMPAIGN_STATUS_LABELS: Record<CampaignStatus, string> = {\n  draft: 'Draft',\n  ready: 'Ready',\n  sending: 'Sending',\n  completed: 'Completed',\n  failed: 'Failed',\n}\n\nexport const MESSAGE_STATUS_LABELS: Record<MessageStatus, string> = {\n  queued: 'Queued',\n  sending: 'Sending',\n  sent: 'Sent',\n  retrying: 'Retrying',\n  failed: 'Failed',\n}\n\n/** Message states in the order of the segmented progress bar and its legend. */\nexport const MESSAGE_STATUS_ORDER: readonly MessageStatus[] = ['sent', 'sending', 'queued', 'retrying', 'failed']\n\nexport const CHANNEL_LABELS: Record<Channel, string> = {\n  sms: 'SMS',\n  email: 'Email',\n}\n\nexport const TONE_LABELS: Record<Tone, string> = {\n  friendly: 'Friendly',\n  professional: 'Professional',\n  playful: 'Playful',\n  urgent: 'Urgent',\n}\n\n/** The card label of a draft state; \"fixing\" names the attempt (\"Fixing… · attempt 2 of 3\"). */\nexport function draftStateLabel(state: DraftState, attempt: number, maxAttempts: number): string {\n  switch (state) {\n    case 'writing':\n      return 'Writing…'\n    case 'validating':\n      return 'Validating…'\n    case 'fixing':\n      return `Fixing… · attempt ${attempt} of ${maxAttempts}`\n    case 'valid':\n      return 'Valid'\n    case 'needs_manual_edit':\n      return 'Needs manual edit'\n  }\n}\n"
}

 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// Primary (gradient), secondary (glass) and ghost buttons (DESIGN.md section 5).\n// While loading the button keeps its label, shows a spinner and cannot be\n// pressed again, so a slow request is never sent twice from the UI.\nimport { computed } from 'vue'\nimport type { RouteLocationRaw } from 'vue-router'\nimport AppIcon, { type IconName } from './AppIcon.vue'\n\nconst props = withDefaults(\n  defineProps<{\n    variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost'\n    type?: 'button' | 'submit'\n    disabled?: boolean\n    loading?: boolean\n    icon?: IconName\n    /** Renders a router link instead of a button. */\n    to?: RouteLocationRaw\n  }>(),\n  { variant: 'secondary', type: 'button', disabled: false, loading: false, icon: undefined, to: undefined },\n)\n\nconst inactive = computed(() => props.disabled || props.loading)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <RouterLink v-if=\"to !== undefined\" :to=\"to\" class=\"btn\" :class=\"`btn-${variant}`\">\n    <AppIcon v-if=\"icon\" :name=\"icon\" />\n    <span><slot /></span>\n  </RouterLink>\n  <button\n    v-else\n    class=\"btn\"\n    :class=\"`btn-${variant}`\"\n    :type=\"type\"\n    :disabled=\"inactive\"\n    :aria-busy=\"loading || undefined\"\n  >\n    <AppIcon v-if=\"loading\" name=\"spinner\" />\n    <AppIcon v-else-if=\"icon\" :name=\"icon\" />\n    <span><slot /></span>\n  </button>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.btn {\n  position: relative;\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: var(--space-2);\n  min-height: var(--control-height);\n  padding: 0 var(--space-6);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  border: var(--border-thin) solid transparent;\n  font-size: var(--font-size-body);\n  font-weight: var(--font-weight-label);\n  line-height: var(--line-height-body);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  white-space: nowrap;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition:\n    box-shadow var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui),\n    background-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);\n}\n\n.btn:disabled {\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n  opacity: var(--opacity-disabled);\n}\n\n.btn[aria-busy='true'] {\n  cursor: progress;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n/* Primary: the AA gradient keeps white text at 4.5:1; the gloss stays above the text. */\n.btn-primary {\n  color: var(--color-white);\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--color-primary-aa-start), var(--color-primary-aa-end));\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card);\n}\n\n.btn-primary::before {\n  content: '';\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0 0 60% 0;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill) var(--radius-pill) 0 0;\n  background: linear-gradient(to bottom, rgb(255 255 255 / 22%), transparent);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:not(:disabled):hover {\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-raised);\n}\n\n.btn-secondary {\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  background: var(--color-glass);\n  border-color: var(--color-border-control);\n}\n\n.btn-secondary:not(:disabled):hover {\n  background: var(--color-glass-hover);\n}\n\n.btn-ghost {\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  background: transparent;\n  padding: 0 var(--space-3);\n}\n\n.btn-ghost:not(:disabled):hover {\n  background: var(--color-glass);\n}\n</style>\n"
}

 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/composables/useConfig.ts",
 "content": "// Which LLM answers (GET /api/config), for the \"Demo mode\" badge. Read once and\n// shared by every component; the mode is fixed while the api runs.\nimport { readonly, ref } from 'vue'\nimport { fetchConfig } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport type { AppConfig } from '@/api/types'\n\nconst config = ref<AppConfig | null>(null)\nlet loading: Promise<void> | null = null\n\nexport function useConfig() {\n  if (!loading) {\n    loading = fetchConfig()\n      .then((c) => {\n        config.value = c\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        // Without the answer the badge stays hidden; the next caller tries again.\n        loading = null\n      })\n  }\n  return readonly(config)\n}\n"
}

})

export default router

import { createApp } from 'vue'
import App from './App.vue'
import router from './router'
// Fonts are self-hosted from npm (no request to Google Fonts). Each package's
// CSS lists one file per script subset with a unicode-range, so the browser
// downloads only the subsets a page uses (latin and latin-ext for Turkish).
import '@fontsource-variable/inter'
import '@fontsource-variable/urbanist'
import '@fontsource-variable/jetbrains-mono'
import './assets/theme.css'
import './assets/base.css'

const app = createApp(App)

app.use(router)

app.mount('#app')
EOF

cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && mkdir -p src/components/ui/__tests__ src/api/__tests__ && cat > src/components/ui/__tests__/StatusPill.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
import StatusPill from '../StatusPill.vue'
import type { CampaignStatus, MessageStatus } from '@/api/types'

describe('StatusPill', () => {
  // The design's exact labels (DESIGN.md section 5): never "Delivered" or "Awaiting approval".
  const campaign: [CampaignStatus, string][] = [
    ['draft', 'Draft'],
    ['ready', 'Ready'],
    ['sending', 'Sending'],
    ['completed', 'Completed'],
    ['failed', 'Failed'],
  ]
  const message: [MessageStatus, string][] = [
    ['queued', 'Queued'],
    ['sending', 'Sending'],
    ['sent', 'Sent'],
    ['retrying', 'Retrying'],
    ['failed', 'Failed'],
  ]

  it.each(campaign)('campaign status %s reads "%s" in its own colors', (status, label) => {
    const pill = mount(StatusPill, { props: { kind: 'campaign', status } })
    expect(pill.text()).toBe(label)
    expect(pill.attributes('style')).toContain(`--color-campaign-${status}-ink`)
  })

  it.each(message)('message state %s reads "%s" in its own colors', (status, label) => {
    const pill = mount(StatusPill, { props: { kind: 'message', status } })
    expect(pill.text()).toBe(label)
    expect(pill.attributes('style')).toContain(`--color-message-${status}-ink`)
  })
})
EOF
cat > src/api/__tests__/client.spec.ts <<'EOF'
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { ApiError, isUnreachable, requestJSON } from '../client'

function respond(status: number, body: string, contentType = 'application/json'): Response {
  return new Response(body, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': contentType } })
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

describe('requestJSON', () => {
  it('sends JSON and returns the parsed answer', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn<typeof fetch>().mockResolvedValue(respond(201, '{"id":7}'))
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)

    await expect(requestJSON('POST', '/api/campaigns', { brief: 'x' })).resolves.toEqual({ id: 7 })
    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0]!
    expect(url).toBe('/api/campaigns')
    expect(init?.method).toBe('POST')
    expect(init?.body).toBe('{"brief":"x"}')
    expect(new Headers(init?.headers).get('Content-Type')).toBe('application/json')
  })

  it('keeps the whole body of a 422 so a validation report can be shown', async () => {
    const body = { error: 'invalid content', report: { valid: false, errors: [{ field: 'text', code: 'too_long' }] } }
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn<typeof fetch>().mockResolvedValue(respond(422, JSON.stringify(body))))

    const err = await requestJSON('PUT', '/api/campaigns/1/messages/sms', { text: 'x' }).catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError)
    expect((err as ApiError).status).toBe(422)
    expect((err as ApiError).message).toBe('invalid content')
    expect((err as ApiError).body).toEqual(body)
    expect(isUnreachable(err)).toBe(false)
  })

  it("treats nginx's 502 page and a failed network as an unreachable server", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn<typeof fetch>().mockResolvedValue(respond(502, '<html>Bad Gateway</html>', 'text/html')))
    const bad = await requestJSON('GET', '/api/config').catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect((bad as ApiError).status).toBe(502)
    expect((bad as ApiError).message).toBe('Request failed with status 502')
    expect(isUnreachable(bad)).toBe(true)

    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn<typeof fetch>().mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('Failed to fetch')))
    const down = await requestJSON('GET', '/api/config').catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect((down as ApiError).status).toBe(0)
    expect(isUnreachable(down)).toBe(true)
  })
})
EOF

 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/logic/content.ts",
 "content": "// Message length rules, ported from the backend's content package\n// (backend/internal/content/template.go and validate.go, DECISIONS.md D15) so\n// the counter on screen shows the very number the backend enforces:\n//\n//   - lengths are Unicode code points, like Go's utf8.RuneCountInString;\n//     JavaScript's `str.length` counts UTF-16 units and would count \"𝟑\" twice;\n//   - the limit applies to the worst case: every variable replaced by the\n//     longest value among the channel's recipients (or its fallback when that\n//     is longer);\n//   - text that is not a valid variable (\"{{ first_name }}\", \"{{name}}\", an\n//     unclosed \"{{\") is counted as written, exactly as the backend renders it.\n//\n// The backend stays the authority: every save is validated there again.\n// A test compares these functions with answers recorded from the live api.\n\nimport type { Channel, Field, MessageContent, Values, Variable } from '@/api/types'\nimport { VARIABLES } from '@/api/types'\n\nexport const SMS_LIMIT = 160\nexport const SUBJECT_LIMIT = 80\n\n/** The fields of a channel's message, in display order. */\nexport function fieldsOf(channel: Channel): Field[] {\n  return channel === 'sms' ? ['text'] : ['subject', 'body']\n}\n\n/** The character limit of a field; 0 means none (the email body). */\nexport function fieldLimit(field: Field): number {\n  return field === 'text' ? SMS_LIMIT : field === 'subject' ? SUBJECT_LIMIT : 0\n}\n\n/** Rendered when a recipient's value is empty (content.Fallbacks). */\nexport const FALLBACKS: Record<Variable, string> = {\n  first_name: 'Müşterimiz',\n  last_name: '',\n  city: '',\n}\n\nexport function placeholder(v: Variable): string {\n  return `{{${v}}}`\n}\n\n/** Length in Unicode code points: the string iterator yields code points. */\nexport function charCount(s: string): number {\n  return [...s].length\n}\n\nexport type Token = { kind: 'text'; text: string } | { kind: 'variable'; variable: Variable }\n\nfunction isVariable(name: string): name is Variable {\n  return (VARIABLES as readonly string[]).includes(name)\n}\n\n/**\n * Splits a template into literal text and whitelisted variables, step by step\n * like content.parse: a \"}}\" before any \"{{\", an unclosed \"{{\", and anything\n * between braces that is not exactly a known variable stay literal text.\n */\nexport function parseTemplate(template: string): Token[] {\n  const tokens: Token[] = []\n  let rest = template\n  while (rest !== '') {\n    const open = rest.indexOf('{{')\n    const closeBeforeOpen = rest.indexOf('}}')\n    if (closeBeforeOpen >= 0 && (open < 0 || closeBeforeOpen < open)) {\n      tokens.push({ kind: 'text', text: rest.slice(0, closeBeforeOpen + 2) })\n      rest = rest.slice(closeBeforeOpen + 2)\n      continue\n    }\n    if (open < 0) {\n      tokens.push({ kind: 'text', text: rest })\n      break\n    }\n    if (open > 0) tokens.push({ kind: 'text', text: rest.slice(0, open) })\n    const close = rest.indexOf('}}', open + 2)\n    if (close < 0) {\n      tokens.push({ kind: 'text', text: rest.slice(open) })\n      break\n    }\n    const name = rest.slice(open + 2, close)\n    if (isVariable(name)) {\n      tokens.push({ kind: 'variable', variable: name })\n    } else {\n      tokens.push({ kind: 'text', text: rest.slice(open, close + 2) })\n    }\n    rest = rest.slice(close + 2)\n  }\n  return tokens\n}\n\n/** Fills the variables (empty values use FALLBACKS); invalid text stays as written. */\nexport function render(template: string, values: Values): string {\n  return parseTemplate(template)\n    .map((t) => (t.kind === 'text' ? t.text : values[t.variable] || FALLBACKS[t.variable]))\n    .join('')\n}\n\n/** Per variable, the longer of the audience's longest value and the fallback (worstValues). */\nexport function worstCaseValues(longest: Values): Record<Variable, string> {\n  cons

<details>
<summary>Write — ~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/logic/draft.ts</summary>

```
{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/logic/draft.ts",
 "content": "// What each message card shows while the drafts are written: a reducer over\n// the draft stream's events (docs/API.md, GET /api/campaigns/{id}/draft/stream).\n//\n// Rules from the contract and the design:\n//   - draft.delta carries a field's whole text so far: it replaces, never appends;\n//   - \"fixing\" keeps the last text on screen until the new attempt's first delta,\n//     which then replaces all of the channel's text (DESIGN.md, message cards);\n//   - \"writing\" starts a channel from nothing (also after a reconnect, when the\n//     api writes an unfinished channel again);\n//   - a final state carries the result: the stored message and its report.\n\nimport type {\n  CampaignView,\n  Channel,\n  ChannelResult,\n  DraftDeltaEvent,\n  DraftDoneEvent,\n  DraftErrorEvent,\n  DraftState,\n  Field,\n  MessageContent,\n  MessageView,\n  Report,\n} from '@/api/types'\nimport { fieldsOf } from './content'\n\nexport const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3\n\nexport interface ChannelDraft {\n  /** \"pending\" until the stream says anything about the channel. */\n  state: DraftState | 'pending'\n  attempt: number\n  maxAttempts: number\n  /** The text on screen, per field. */\n  fields: Partial<Record<Field, string>>\n  /** The attempt the text on screen belongs to. */\n  textAttempt: number\n  /** What a fixing attempt corrects, or why a manual edit is needed. */\n  problems: string[]\n  /** The backend's validation of the final message; null until then or without one. */\n  report: Report | null\n}\n\nexport interface DraftProgress {\n  channels: Partial<Record<Channel, ChannelDraft>>\n  errors: string[]\n  done: DraftDoneEvent | null\n}\n\nexport function isFinal(state: ChannelDraft['state']): boolean {\n  return state === 'valid' || state === 'needs_manual_edit'\n}\n\nfunction pending(): ChannelDraft {\n  return {\n    state: 'pending',\n    attempt: 0,\n    maxAttempts: MAX_ATTEMPTS,\n    fields: {},\n    textAttempt: 0,\n    problems: [],\n    report: null,\n  }\n}\n\nfunction fieldsFrom(channel: Channel, message: MessageContent | null): Partial<Record<Field, string>> {\n  if (!message) return {}\n  const fields: Partial<Record<Field, string>> = {}\n  for (const f of fieldsOf(channel)) fields[f] = message[f] ?? ''\n  return fields\n}\n\nfunction finalDraft(\n  channel: Channel,\n  r: Pick<ChannelResult, 'state' | 'attempts' | 'message' | 'report' | 'problems'>,\n): ChannelDraft {\n  return {\n    state: r.state,\n    attempt: r.attempts,\n    maxAttempts: MAX_ATTEMPTS,\n    fields: fieldsFrom(channel, r.message),\n    textAttempt: r.attempts,\n    problems: r.problems,\n    report: r.report,\n  }\n}\n\n/** A stored message as a finished card. */\nexport function fromMessageView(channel: Channel, m: MessageView): ChannelDraft {\n  return finalDraft(channel, m)\n}\n\n/** The cards before the stream: stored channels are final, the others pending. */\nexport function initialProgress(view: CampaignView): DraftProgress {\n  const channels: DraftProgress['channels'] = {}\n  for (const ch of view.channels) {\n    const m = view.messages[ch]\n    channels[ch] = m ? fromMessageView(ch, m) : pending()\n  }\n  return { channels, errors: [], done: null }\n}\n\n/** True when some selected channel has no stored message yet, i.e. the stream must run. */\nexport function needsDrafting(view: CampaignView): boolean {\n  return view.channels.some((ch) => !view.messages[ch])\n}\n\nexport interface DraftStateData {\n  channel: Channel\n  attempt: number\n  state: DraftState\n  max_attempts?: number\n  problems?: string[]\n  result?: ChannelResult\n}\n\nexport type DraftEvent =\n  | { type: 'draft.state'; data: DraftStateData }\n  | { type: 'draft.delta'; data: DraftDeltaEvent }\n  | { type: 'draft.error'; data: DraftErrorEvent }\n  | { type: 'draft.done'; data: DraftDoneEvent }\n\n/** Applies one stream event and returns the new progress (the o

It is rounded, except that\n * it never reads 100% before every message is final (231 of 232 is 99%).\n */\nexport function progressPercent(done: number, total: number): number {\n  if (total <= 0) return 0\n  if (done >= total) return 100\n  return Math.min(99, Math.round((done / total) * 100))\n}\n\nexport interface Segment {\n  status: MessageStatus\n  count: number\n  /** Exact share of the channel's messages, for the bar's width (not rounded). */\n  percent: number\n}\n\n/** The segmented bar's parts in the design's order: Sent, Sending, Queued, Retrying, Failed. */\nexport function segments(ch: ChannelSnapshot): Segment[] {\n  return MESSAGE_STATUS_ORDER.map((status) => ({\n    status,\n    count: ch[status],\n    percent: ch.total > 0 ? (ch[status] / ch.total) * 100 : 0,\n  }))\n}\n\n/** How full the rate gauge is: rate ÷ limit, between 0 and 1. */\nexport function gaugeFraction(rate: number, limit: number): number {\n  if (limit <= 0) return 0\n  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, rate / limit))\n}\n\nconst KIND_LABELS: Record<Issue['kind'], string> = {\n  throttled: 'Provider busy',\n  provider_error: 'Provider error',\n  rejected: 'Rejected',\n  no_answer: 'No answer',\n}\n\n/**\n * The sentence of a \"Recent issues\" row (D25 K4: the backend classifies, the\n * UI words it).  "content": "// A server-sent-events stream that comes back by itself (DECISIONS.md D25,\n// docs/API.md \"Reconnecting\").\n//\n// EventSource handles the easy case: when an open stream simply ends (the api\n// shut down), it reconnects after the server's `retry: 3000`. But when an\n// answer is not 200 (nginx's 502 while the api restarts, or a 404/409), it\n// gives up for good: readyState becomes CLOSED and it never tries again. It\n// does not tell us the status code either. So on CLOSED we wait (backoff with\n// jitter), let the caller check what happened (e.g. GET the campaign), and\n// open a new EventSource unless the caller says stop.\n//\n// The stream is over only after its done event (draft.done, delivery.done);\n// then we close it ourselves, because EventSource would reopen a stream that\n// ends. Optionally, silence for longer than `stallMs` counts as a lost\n// connection (a laptop waking up, a half-open TCP connection).\n\n/** What the screen shows about the connection. */\nexport type ConnectionState = 'connecting' | 'live' | 'reconnecting' | 'closed'\n\n/** The part of EventSource we use; tests pass a fake. */\nexport interface EventSourceLike {\n  readonly readyState: number\n  onerror: ((ev: Event) => void) | null\n  addEventListener(type: string, listener: (ev: MessageEvent) => void): void\n  close(): void\n}\n\nexport type EventSourceFactory = (url: string) => EventSourceLike\n\n/** EventSource.CLOSED; the constant is not available in every test environment. */\nconst CLOSED = 2\n\nexport interface Backoff {\n  firstMs: number\n  maxMs: number\n  /** ± share of the delay, so many browsers do not come back at the same instant. */\n  jitter: number\n}\n\nexport const DEFAULT_BACKOFF: Backoff = { firstMs: 1000, maxMs: 10_000, jitter: 0.2 }\n\n/** Delay before reconnect number `failures` (0-based): 1, 2, 4, 8, 10, 10 … s, ± jitter. */\nexport function backoffDelay(failures: number, b: Backoff, random: () => number): number {\n  const base = Math.min(b.maxMs, b.firstMs * 2 ** failures)\n  return Math.round(base * (1 + b.jitter * (2 * random() - 1)))\n}\n\nexport interface StreamOptions {\n  url: string\n  /** Handlers of named events, called with the event's parsed JSON. */\n  handlers: Record<string, (data: unknown) => void>\n  /** The event that ends the stream for good. */\n  doneEvent: string\n  onState: (state: ConnectionState) => void\n  /** After the browser gave up (CLOSED): \"retry\" opens the stream again, \"stop\" ends it. */\n  check?: () => Promise<'retry' | 'stop'>\n  /** Reconnect when no event arrived for this long; Rejected edits\n// (422) store nothing; the report comes back either way.\n\nimport { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'\n\nconst base = process.env.API_URL ?? 'http://localhost:8080'\nconst out = new URL('../src/logic/__tests__/fixtures/worst-case.recorded.json', import.meta.url)\n\nasync function call(method, path, body) {\n  const res = await fetch(base + path, {\n    method,\n    headers: body === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),\n  })\n  return { status: res.status, json: await res.json() }\n}\n\n// Deterministic random numbers (mulberry32), so a re-recording sends the same templates.\nfunction rng(seed) {\n  let a = seed\n  return () => {\n    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0\n    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a)\n    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t\n    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296\n  }\n}\n\n// Edge cases of the backend's parser and counter, then random mixes of the same pieces.\nconst handPicked = [\n  '',\n  'Merhaba {{first_name}}, sepetindeki ürünler seni bekliyor!',\n  '{{first_name}} {{last_name}} {{city}}',\n  '{{last_name}}{{last_name}}{{last_name}}',\n  'Merhaba {{ first_name }}',\n  'Merhaba {{First_Name}}',\n  'Merhaba {{name}} ve {{first_name}}',\n  'Merhaba {{first_name}',\n  'Merhaba first_name}} ve {{city}}',\n  '}}{{first_name}}{{',\n  '{{{first_name}}}',\n  '{{}}',\n  'İndirim {{%30}} {{city}}',\n  'Tek parantez {first_name} değişken değildir',\n  'Toplam 𝟑𝟎 indirim {{first_name}}',\n  'é ve ş ğ ı İ ü ö ç â',\n  'a'.repeat(147) + '{{first_name}}',\n  'a'.repeat(148) + '{{first_name}}',\n  'ş'.repeat(160),\n  'ğ'.repeat(161),\n]\nconst pieces = [\n  'a', 'ş', 'İ', ' ', '%30', '!', 'ğü', 'â', 'é', '𝟑',\n  '{{first_name}}', '{{last_name}}', '{{city}}',\n  '{{', '}}', '{', '}', '{{ city }}', '{{name}}', '{{first_name}',\n]\nconst random = rng(20261021)\nconst generated = []\nfor (let i = 0;  "content": "import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'\nimport {\n  backoffDelay,\n  openStream,\n  type ConnectionState,\n  type EventSourceLike,\n  type StreamOptions,\n} from '../stream'\n\nconst CONNECTING = 0\nconst OPEN = 1\nconst CLOSED = 2\n\n/** A stand-in for the browser's EventSource that the test drives by hand. */\nclass FakeEventSource implements EventSourceLike {\n  readyState = CONNECTING\n  onerror: ((ev: Event) => void) | null = null\n  closed = false\n  private listeners = new Map<string, ((ev: MessageEvent) => void)[]>()\n\n  constructor(public readonly url: string) {}\n\n  addEventListener(type: string, listener: (ev: MessageEvent) => void) {\n    this.listeners.set(type, [...(this.listeners.get(type) ?? []), listener])\n  }\n\n  close() {\n    this.closed = true\n    this.readyState = CLOSED\n  }\n\n  emit(type: string, data: unknown) {\n    this.readyState = OPEN\n    for (const l of this.listeners.get(type) ?? []) {\n      l(new MessageEvent(type, { data: JSON.stringify(data) }))\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** The browser lost an open stream and is reconnecting by itself. */\n  dropAndRetry() {\n    this.readyState = CONNECTING\n    this.onerror?.(new Event('error'))\n  }\n\n  /** The browser gave up for good (a 502 from nginx, a 404, a 409). */\n  fail() {\n    this.readyState = CLOSED\n    this.onerror?.(new Event('error'))\n  }\n}\n\nlet sources: FakeEventSource[]\nlet states: ConnectionState[]\nlet received: unknown[]\n\nfunction open(extra: Partial<StreamOptions> = {}) {\n  return openStream({\n    url: '/api/campaigns/1/events',\n    handlers: { 'delivery.snapshot': (d) => received.push(d) },\n    doneEvent: 'delivery.done',\n    onState: (s) => states.push(s),\n    random: () => 0.5, // no jitter: delays are exactly 1 s, 2 s, 4 s …\n    createEventSource: (url) => {\n      const es = new FakeEventSource(url)\n      sources.push(es)\n      return es\n    },\n    ...extra,\n  })\n}\n\nconst last = () => sources[sources.length - 1]!\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.useFakeTimers()\n  sources = []\n  states = []\n  received = []\n})\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.useRealTimers()\n})\n\ndescribe('openStream', () => {\n  it('passes parsed events on and reports the connection as live', () => {\n    open()\n    expect(states).toEqual(['connecting'])\n    last().emit('delivery.snapshot', { done: 1 })\n    last().emit('delivery.snapshot', { done: 2 })\n    expect(received).toEqual([{ done: 1 }, { done: 2 }])\n    expect(states.at(-1)).toBe('live')\n  })\n\n  it('closes the stream on the done event and never reopens it', async () => {\n    const done: unknown[] = []\n    open({ handlers: { 'delivery.done': (d) => done.push(d) } })\n    last().emit('delivery.done', { campaign_id: 1, status: 'completed' })\n    expect(done).toEqual([{ campaign_id: 1, status: 'completed' }])\n    expect(last().closed).toBe(true)\n    expect(states.at(-1)).toBe('closed')\n\n    last().fail()\n    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000)\n    expect(sources).toHaveLength(1)\n  })\n\n  it('leaves a dropped stream to the browser while it is reconnecting by itself', async () => {\n    open()\n    last().emit('delivery.snapshot', {})\n    last().dropAndRetry()\n    expect(states.at(-1)).toBe('reconnecting')\n    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000)\n    expect(sources).toHaveLength(1)\n  })\n\n  it('reopens the stream itself after the browser gave up, with growing waits', async () => {\n    const check = vi.fn(async () => 'retry' as const)\n    open({ check })\n    last().fail() // e.g. nginx answered 502 while the api restarts\n    expect(states.at(-1)).toBe('reconnecting')\n\n    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(999)\n    expect(sources).toHaveLength(1)\n    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1)\n    expect(check).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)\n    expect(sources).toHaveLength(2)\n\n    last().fail()\n

import re
p = "src/logic/__tests__/stream.spec.ts"
s = open(p).read()
n = s.count("states.at(-1)")
s = s.replace("states.at(-1)", "lastState()")
s = s.replace("const last = () => sources[sources.length - 1]!\n",
              "const last = () => sources[sources.length - 1]!\nconst lastState = () => states[states.length - 1]\n")
a = "const check = vi.fn(async () => 'retry' as const)"
assert s.count(a) == 1
s = s.replace(a, "const check = vi.fn<() => Promise<'retry'>>(async () => 'retry')")
b = "const check = vi.fn(() => new Promise<'retry'>((r) => (answer = r)))"
assert s.count(b) == 1
s = s.replace(b, "const check = vi.fn<() => Promise<'retry'>>(\n      () => new Promise<'retry'>((r) => (answer = r)),\n    )")
open(p, "w").write(s)

p = "scripts/record-worst-case.mjs"
s = open(p).read()
old = """async function call(method, path, body) {
  const res = await fetch(base + path, {
    method,
    headers: body === undefined ? {} : { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  })
  return { status: res.status, json: await res.json() }
}"""
new = """async function call(method, path, body) {
  const init = { method }
  if (body !== undefined) {
    init.headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
    init.body = JSON.stringify(body)
  }
  const res = await fetch(base + path, init)
  return { status: res.status, json: await res.json() }
}"""
assert s.count(old) == 1, "script"
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF

<details>
<summary>Bash — Commit step S2</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && git add -A frontend && git status --short && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
feat(frontend): draft, counter and live-stream logic

Plain TypeScript under src/logic, independent of Vue, so it is tested
directly:

- content.ts: the backend's template parser, renderer and worst-case
  length ported from internal/content. Lengths are code points
  ([...s].length), not UTF-16 units; invalid variables count as written;
  near limit from 90% (144/160, 72/80)
- draft.ts: reducer of the draft stream's events: deltas replace, fixing
  keeps the old text until the new attempt writes, a final state carries
  the stored message and its report
- snapshot.ts: dashboard numbers (progress that never reads 100% early,
  segment shares, gauge), the "Recent issues" wording from kind/outcome/
  retry_in_ms, issue age against the snapshot's own clock, and a monotone
  curve (Steffen tangents) that never draws above the highest sample
- stream.ts: EventSource with our own reconnect: the browser's retry when
  a stream ends, our backoff (1-10 s, jitter) plus a caller check when the
  browser gives up after a 502/404/409, a stall watchdog, close on the
  done event

The counter test compares content.ts with 720 answers recorded from the
live api (mock LLM) by scripts/record-worst-case.mjs: templates with
broken variables, Turkish letters, combining marks and characters outside
the BMP, two audiences, SMS text and email subject.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
git log --oneline -1 && git status --porcelain | wc -l

{
 "file_path": "/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-user-Desktop-Smart-Campagin-Composer-Case/c3e7c4d9-6939-462d-8872-d00d21388b2b/scratchpad/mutate.py",
 "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Mutation checks for the frontend (same procedure as P4/P5).\n\nFor each mutant: the tree must be clean, the replacement must apply exactly\nonce, the mutant must pass `vue-tsc --build` and `vite build` (otherwise the\nresult is INVALID, never \"caught\"), then the whole Vitest suite runs unfiltered\nand its full log is kept. Afterwards the file is restored from a backup,\ncompared with `cmp`, and `git status` must be clean again.\n\nusage: mutate.py <repo> <mutants.json> <logdir>\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport pathlib\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nrepo = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])\nfront = repo / \"frontend\"\nmutants = json.loads(pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text())\nlogdir = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[3])\nlogdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n\ndef run(cmd, cwd, log=None):\n    p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True)\n    out = p.stdout + p.stderr\n    if log:\n        log.write_text(out)\n    return p.returncode, out\n\n\ndef clean():\n    _, out = run([\"git\", \"status\", \"--porcelain\"], repo)\n    return out.strip() == \"\"\n\n\nresults = []\nfor i, m in enumerate(mutants, 1):\n    label, rel, old, new = m[\"label\"], m[\"file\"], m[\"old\"], m[\"new\"]\n    path = front / rel\n    tag = f\"{i:02d}\"\n    if not clean():\n        print(f\"[{tag}] tree not clean before the mutant; stopping\")\n        sys.exit(2)\n    src = path.read_text()\n    if src.count(old) != 1:\n        results.append((tag, label, f\"INVALID: replacement found {src.count(old)} times\"))\n        continue\n    backup = logdir / f\"{tag}.backup\"\n    shutil.copyfile(path, backup)\n    path.write_text(src.replace(old, new))\n    try:\n        _, diff = run([\"git\", \"diff\", \"--\", str(path)], repo)\n        (logdir / f\"{tag}.diff\").write_text(diff)\n        print(f\"===== [{tag}] {label}\\n{diff}\")\n        tsc, _ = run([\"npx\", \"vue-tsc\", \"--build\"], front, logdir / f\"{tag}.tsc.log\")\n        build, _ = run([\"npx\", \"vite\", \"build\"], front, logdir / f\"{tag}.build.log\")\n        if tsc != 0 or build != 0:\n            results.append((tag, label, f\"INVALID: mutant does not build (vue-tsc {tsc}, vite build {build})\"))\n            continue\n        code, out = run([\"npx\", \"vitest\", \"--run\"], front, logdir / f\"{tag}.vitest.log\")\n        failing = sorted({l.strip() for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip().startswith(\"FAIL \")})\n        summary = [l.strip() for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip().startswith(\"Tests \")]\n        if code == 0:\n            results.append((tag, label, \"SURVIVED (all tests pass)\"))\n        else:\n            results.append((tag, label, f\"caught, exit {code}; {summary[-1] if summary else '?'}\\n      \" + \"\\n      \".join(failing)))\n    finally:\n        shutil.copyfile(backup, path)\n        same = subprocess.run([\"cmp\", str(backup), str(path)]).returncode == 0\n        if not same or not clean():\n            print(f\"[{tag}] restore failed (cmp same={same}); stopping\")\n            sys.exit(3)\n\nprint(\"\\n===== SUMMARY\")\nfor tag, label, res in results:\n    print(f\"[{tag}] {label}: {res}\")\nprint(\"tree clean:\", clean())\n"
}

// Create turns a brief into a draft campaign: the model proposes the
// audience rule and a name, the backend validates the rule (with repairs),
// and the draft is stored. Messages are written by StreamDrafts.
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, in CreateInput) (View, error) {
	if err := in.normalize(); err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	asOf := s.Now().UTC()
	res, err := s.Generator.Segment(ctx, s.Registry, in.Brief)
	if err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	s.Logger.Info("audience rule ready", "attempts", res.Attempts,
		"input_tokens", res.Usage.InputTokens, "output_tokens", res.Usage.OutputTokens, "cache_read_tokens", res.Usage.CacheReadTokens)

	c := Campaign{
		Name: res.CampaignName, Brief: in.Brief, Tone: in.Tone, Channels: in.Channels,
		Rule: res.Rule, UnsupportedCriteria: res.UnsupportedCriteria, AsOf: asOf,
		LLMMode: s.LLMMode, LLMModel: s.LLMModel,
	}
	if err := s.Repo.Create(ctx, &c); err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	return s.Get(ctx, c.ID)
}

// Get returns a campaign as the UI shows it.
func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, id int64) (View, error) {
	c, err := s.Repo.Get(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	v, longest, err := s.audience(ctx, c)
	if err != nil {
		return View{}, err
	}
	return s.view(c, v, longest), nil
}

// List returns the most recent campaigns with their audience size.
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, limit int) ([]Summary, error) {
	cs, err := s.Repo.List(ctx, limit)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Summary, 0, len(cs))
	for _, c := range cs {
		v, err := segment.ParseAndValidate(c.Rule, s.Registry)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("campaign %d: stored rule no longer valid: %w", c.ID, err)
		}
		a, err := s.Audience.Count(ctx, v, c.AsOf)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, Summary{ID: c.ID, Name: c.Name, Status: c.Status, Channels: c.Channels,
			Audience: a.Reach(c.Channels), CreatedAt: c.CreatedAt})
	}
	return out, nil
}

// audience re-validates the stored rule and measures it at the campaign's
// anchor time.
func (s *Service) audience(ctx context.Context, c Campaign) (audienceView, map[content.Channel]content.Values, error) {
	v, err := segment.ParseAndValidate(c.Rule, s.Registry)
	if err != nil {
		return audienceView{}, nil, fmt.Errorf("campaign %d: stored rule no longer valid: %w", c.ID, err)
	}
	a, err := s.Audience.Count(ctx, v, c.AsOf)
	if err != nil {
		return audienceView{}, nil, err
	}

package campaign

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/content"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/llm"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/llm/mock"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/logging"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/seed"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/segment"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/testdb"
	"github.com/Berke-omr/smart-campaign-composer/backend/internal/users"
)

var asOf = time.Date(2026, 10, 19, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

type fixture struct {
	pool    *pgxpool.Pool
	svc     *Service
	mock    *mock.Client
	catalog *mock.Catalog
}

func setup(t *testing.T, opts mock.Options) *fixture {
	t.Helper()
	pool := testdb.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := seed.Load(ctx, pool, seed.Generate(5000, 42, asOf), false); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cities, err := users.NewRepository(pool).DistinctCities(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cat, err := mock.LoadCatalog()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	opts.Cities = cities
	m := mock.New(cat, opts)
	svc := &Service{
		Repo:      NewRepository(pool),
		Generator: &llm.Generator{Client: m, AttemptTimeout: 100 * time.Millisecond},
		Registry:  segment.NewRegistry(cities),
		Audience:  segment.NewCounter(pool),
		LLMMode:   "mock",
		Logger:    logging.Discard(),
		Now:       func() time.Time { return asOf },
	}
	return &fixture{pool: pool, svc: svc, mock: m, catalog: cat}
}

func (f *fixture) brief(t *testing.T, id string) CreateInput {
	t.Helper()
	s, ok := f.catalog.Scenario(id)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("no scenario %s", id)
	}
	return CreateInput{Brief: s.Brief, Channels: s.Channels, Tone: s.Tone}
}

type sse struct {
	event string
	data  any
}

type recorder struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	events []sse
}

func (r *recorder) emit(event string, data any) error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.events = append(r.events, sse{event, data})
	return nil
}

func (r *recorder) terminal() map[content.Channel]llm.Event {
	out := map[content.Channel]llm.Event{}
	for _, e := range r.events {
		if ev, ok := e.data.(llm.Event); ok && ev.State.Terminal() {
			out[ev.Channel] = ev
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (r *recorder) last() sse { return r.events[len(r.events)-1] }

func 
… [3130 more characters truncated]
