<details>
<summary>Bash — Commit step S3b</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && git add -A backend docs/API.md frontend/src/api/types.ts && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
feat(api): longest values per channel in the campaign view

The UI's counter must show the number the validator enforces (D15), which
needs the values the worst case is computed with. Until now they came only
inside a message's report, so there were none while a message streamed
and none for a channel whose draft never produced content (declined twice,
timed out). The campaign view's audience now carries `longest_values` per
selected channel: content.WorstCaseValues of the channel's longest values,
the same values every report of that channel uses. Additive; no existing
field changes.

TestViewHasLongestValuesPerChannel: equal to the email report's values,
equal to what the validator uses for an SMS without content, the
fallback when every name is shorter than it, and only selected channels.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
git log --oneline -1; git status --porcelain | wc -l

<details>
<summary>Write — ~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/components/ui/AppDialog.vue</summary>

```
{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/components/ui/AppDialog.vue",
 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// Modal dialog on the native <dialog> element: showModal() makes the rest of\n// the page inert (focus stays inside) and Escape fires \"cancel\". The parent\n// owns `open`; Escape or the close button ask it to close. Focus goes back to\n// the element that opened the dialog. While `busy`, it cannot be dismissed,\n// so a request in flight is never abandoned halfway.\nimport { nextTick, onMounted, ref, useId, watch } from 'vue'\nimport AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue'\n\nconst props = withDefaults(defineProps<{ open: boolean; title: string; busy?: boolean }>(), {\n  busy: false,\n})\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ close: [] }>()\n\nconst el = ref<HTMLDialogElement | null>(null)\nconst titleId = useId()\nlet opener: HTMLElement | null = null\n\nfunction sync(open: boolean) {\n  const d = el.value\n  if (!d) return\n  if (open && !d.open) {\n    opener = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null\n    d.showModal()\n  } else if (!open && d.open) {\n    d.close()\n    void nextTick(() => opener?.focus())\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(() => sync(props.open))\nwatch(\n  () => props.open,\n  (open) => sync(open),\n  { flush: 'post' },\n)\n\nfunction requestClose() {\n  if (!props.busy) emit('close')\n}\n\nfunction onCancel(e: Event) {\n  e.preventDefault() // the parent decides; it may be busy\n  requestClose()\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <dialog ref=\"el\" class=\"dialog glass\" :aria-labelledby=\"titleId\" @cancel=\"onCancel\">\n    <header class=\"dialog-header\">\n      <h2 :id=\"titleId\" class=\"dialog-title\">{{ title }}</h2>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        class=\"dialog-close\"\n        aria-label=\"Close\"\n        :disabled=\"busy\"\n        @click=\"requestClose\"\n      >\n        <AppIcon name=\"close\" />\n      </button>\n    </header>\n    <div class=\"dialog-body\"><slot /></div>\n    <footer v-if=\"$slots.footer\" class=\"dialog-footer\"><slot name=\"footer\" /></footer>\n  </dialog>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.dialog {\n  width: min(40rem, calc(100vw - 2 * var(--space-6)));\n  max-height: calc(100vh - 2 * var(--space-8));\n  padding: var(--space-6);\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  background: var(--color-glass-hover);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-raised), var(--shadow-highlight);\n  overflow: auto;\n}\n\n.dialog::backdrop {\n  background: var(--color-overlay);\n}\n\n.dialog[open] {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: var(--space-5);\n}\n\n.dialog-header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: flex-start;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: var(--space-4);\n}\n\n.dialog-close {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: var(--icon-button-size);\n  height: var(--icon-button-size);\n  margin: calc(-1 * var(--space-2));\n  border: 0;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  background: transparent;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.dialog-close:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--color-glass);\n}\n\n.dialog-close:disabled {\n  opacity: var(--opacity-disabled);\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.dialog-body {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: var(--space-4);\n}\n\n.dialog-footer {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: flex-end;\n  gap: var(--space-3);\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n}\n</style>\n"
}

With `pressed` it is a toggle button (aria-pressed); with\n// `action` a plain button; otherwise static text that is not focusable.\n// A channel chip shows the channel's dot and, when pressed, its tint.\nimport { computed } from 'vue'\nimport type { Channel } from '@/api/types'\nimport AppIcon, { type IconName } from './AppIcon.vue'\n\nconst props = withDefaults(\n  defineProps<{\n    pressed?: boolean\n    action?: boolean\n    channel?: Channel\n    icon?: IconName\n    disabled?: boolean\n  }>(),\n  { pressed: undefined, action: false, channel: undefined, icon: undefined, disabled: false },\n)\ndefineEmits<{ click: [MouseEvent] }>()\n\nconst interactive = computed(() => props.pressed !== undefined || props.action)\nconst tinted = computed(() => props.channel !== undefined && props.pressed === true)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <button\n    v-if=\"interactive\"\n    type=\"button\"\n    class=\"chip chip-interactive\"\n    :class=\"{ tinted }\"\n    :aria-pressed=\"pressed === undefined ? undefined : pressed\"\n    :disabled=\"disabled\"\n    :style=\"channel ? { '--chip-channel': `var(--color-${channel})`, '--chip-tint': `var(--color-${channel}-tint)`, '--chip-border': `var(--color-${channel}-border)`, '--chip-ink': `var(--color-${channel}-ink)` } : undefined\"\n    @click=\"$emit('click', $event)\"\n  >\n    <span v-if=\"channel\" class=\"chip-dot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n    <AppIcon v-else-if=\"icon\" :name=\"icon\" :size=\"18\" />\n    <slot />\n  </button>\n  <span v-else class=\"chip\">\n    <span\n      v-if=\"channel\"\n      class=\"chip-dot\"\n      aria-hidden=\"true\"\n      :style=\"{ '--chip-channel': `var(--color-${channel})` }\"\n    ></span>\n    <AppIcon v-else-if=\"icon\" :name=\"icon\" :size=\"18\" />\n    <slot />\n  </span>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.chip {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: var(--space-2);\n  min-height: var(--control-height);\n  padding: 0 var(--space-5);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  border: var(--border-thin) solid var(--color-glass-border);\n  background: var(--color-glass);\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  font-size: var(--font-size-label);\n  line-height: var(--line-height-label);\n  font-weight: var(--font-weight-label);\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.chip-interactive {\n  border-color: var(--color-border-control);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);\n}\n\n.chip-interactive:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--color-glass-hover);\n}\n\n.chip-interactive:disabled {\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n  opacity: var(--opacity-disabled);\n}\n\n.chip-interactive.tinted {\n  background: var(--chip-tint);\n  border-color: var(--chip-ink);\n  color: var(--chip-ink);\n}\n\n.chip-dot {\n  width: 0.75rem;\n  height: 0.75rem;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  background: var(--chip-channel);\n}\n\n/* An unpressed channel toggle shows a hollow dot, so the state is not color alone. */\n.chip-interactive[aria-pressed='false'] .chip-dot {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 2px solid var(--chip-channel);\n}\n</style>\n"

 "content": "// Vitest setup. jsdom has the <dialog> element but not its methods; this\n// stand-in opens and closes it the way the browser does, without the modal\n// inertness that jsdom cannot emulate anyway.\nif (typeof HTMLDialogElement !== 'undefined' && !HTMLDialogElement.prototype.showModal) {\n  HTMLDialogElement.prototype.showModal = function showModal(this: HTMLDialogElement) {\n    this.setAttribute('open', '')\n  }\n  HTMLDialogElement.prototype.close = function close(this: HTMLDialogElement) {\n    this.removeAttribute('open')\n    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('close'))\n  }\n}\n"
}

import { createRouter, createWebHistory, type RouteRecordRaw } from 'vue-router'
import CampaignDraftView from '@/views/CampaignDraftView.vue'
import ComposerView from '@/views/ComposerView.vue'

/** Campaign ids in the URL are numbers; the views get them as a number prop. */
const idProp = (route: { params: Record<string, unknown> }) => ({ id: Number(route.params.id) })

export const routes: RouteRecordRaw[] = [
  { path: '/', name: 'composer', component: ComposerView },
  { path: '/campaigns/:id(\\d+)', name: 'campaign', component: CampaignDraftView, props: idProp },
  { path: '/:pathMatch(.*)*', redirect: '/' },
]

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(import.meta.env.BASE_URL),
  routes,
})

export default router
EOF
npx vue-tsc --build && echo ok

<script setup lang="ts">
// Audience → Messages → Review → Send. Informational, not navigation: done
// steps show a check, the current one is marked with aria-current="step".
import AppIcon from './AppIcon.vue'

export interface Step {
  label: string
  status: 'done' | 'current' | 'upcoming'
}

defineProps<{ steps: Step[] }>()
</script>

<template>
  <ol class="steps" aria-label="Progress">
    <li
      v-for="(step, i) in steps"
      :key="step.label"
      class="step"
      :class="step.status"
      :aria-current="step.status === 'current' ? 'step' : undefined"
    >
      <span class="marker" aria-hidden="true">
        <AppIcon v-if="step.status === 'done'" name="check" :size="16" />
      </span>
      <span class="label">{{ step.label }}</span>
      <span v-if="step.status === 'done'" class="sr-only">(done)</span>
      <span v-if="i < steps.length - 1" class="line" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    </li>
  </ol>
</template>

<style scoped>
.steps {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
}

.step {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  min-width: 0;
}

.marker {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex: none;
  width: 2rem;
  height: 2rem;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  border: 2px solid var(--color-border-control);
  background: var(--color-glass);
}

.label {
  font-size: var(--font-size-body);
  color: var(--color-text-secondary);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.line {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: var(--space-6);
  height: 2px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--color-track);
}

.done .marker {
  border-color: var(--color-sms);
  background: var(--color-sms);
  color: var(--color-white);
}

.done .label {
  color: var(--color-text);
}

.current .marker {
  border-color: var(--color-primary-aa-start);
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary-start), var(--color-primary-end));
}

.current .label {
  color: var(--color-primary-aa-start);
  font-weight: 600;
}
</style>
EOF

```
{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/composables/useDraftStream.ts",
 "content": "// Runs the draft stream of one campaign (GET /api/campaigns/{id}/draft/stream)\n// and keeps the message cards' state. Stored channels are shown at once; the\n// stream is opened only while some selected channel has no message yet.\nimport { onBeforeUnmount, ref } from 'vue'\nimport { draftStreamURL } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport type {\n  CampaignView,\n  DraftDeltaEvent,\n  DraftDoneEvent,\n  DraftErrorEvent,\n  DraftStateEvent,\n} from '@/api/types'\nimport {\n  applyDraftEvent,\n  initialProgress,\n  needsDrafting,\n  type DraftEvent,\n  type DraftProgress,\n} from '@/logic/draft'\nimport { openStream, type ConnectionState, type StreamHandle } from '@/logic/stream'\n\nexport interface DraftStreamOptions {\n  /** Called once every channel is final (draft.done). */\n  onDone: (done: DraftDoneEvent) => void\n  /** Called when the browser gave up on the stream (e.g. a 502); see openStream. */\n  check: () => Promise<'retry' | 'stop'>\n}\n\nexport function useDraftStream(id: number, options: DraftStreamOptions) {\n  const progress = ref<DraftProgress>({ channels: {}, errors: [], done: null })\n  const connection = ref<ConnectionState>('closed')\n  let handle: StreamHandle | null = null\n\n  function apply(ev: DraftEvent) {\n    progress.value = applyDraftEvent(progress.value, ev)\n  }\n\n  /** Shows the campaign's stored messages and, if some are missing, starts writing them. */\n  function start(view: CampaignView) {\n    handle?.close()\n    handle = null\n    progress.value = { ...initialProgress(view), errors: progress.value.errors }\n    if (!needsDrafting(view) || (view.status !== 'draft' && view.status !== 'ready')) {\n      connection.value = 'closed'\n      return\n    }\n    handle = openStream({\n      url: draftStreamURL(id),\n      handlers: {\n        'draft.state': (d) => apply({ type: 'draft.state', data: d as DraftStateEvent }),\n        'draft.delta': (d) => apply({ type: 'draft.delta', data: d as DraftDeltaEvent }),\n        'draft.error': (d) => apply({ type: 'draft.error', data: d as DraftErrorEvent }),\n        'draft.done': (d) => {\n          apply({ type: 'draft.done', data: d as DraftDoneEvent })\n          options.onDone(d as DraftDoneEvent)\n        },\n      },\n      doneEvent: 'draft.done',\n      onState: (s) => (connection.value = s),\n      check: options.check,\n    })\n  }\n\n  function stop() {\n    handle?.close()\n    handle = null\n  }\n\n  onBeforeUnmount(stop)\n\n  return {\n    progress,\n    connection,\n    start,\n    stop,\n    retryNow: () => handle?.retryNow(),\n  }\n}\n"
}

Approved campaigns open their dashboard.\nimport { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue'\nimport { useRouter } from 'vue-router'\nimport { fetchCampaign } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport { ApiError, isUnreachable } from '@/api/client'\nimport type { CampaignView } from '@/api/types'\nimport AudienceCard from '@/components/campaign/AudienceCard.vue'\nimport MessageCard from '@/components/campaign/MessageCard.vue'\nimport AppBanner from '@/components/ui/AppBanner.vue'\nimport AppButton from '@/components/ui/AppButton.vue'\nimport GlassCard from '@/components/ui/GlassCard.vue'\nimport SkeletonLines from '@/components/ui/SkeletonLines.vue'\nimport StatusPill from '@/components/ui/StatusPill.vue'\nimport StepIndicator, { type Step } from '@/components/ui/StepIndicator.vue'\nimport { useDraftStream } from '@/composables/useDraftStream'\nimport { isFinal, needsDrafting } from '@/logic/draft'\n\nconst props = defineProps<{ id: number }>()\nconst router = useRouter()\n\nconst campaign = ref<CampaignView | null>(null)\nconst loadError = ref<'not_found' | 'unreachable' | 'other' | null>(null)\n\nconst drafts = useDraftStream(props.id, {\n  onDone: () => void refresh(),\n  check: checkAfterDisconnect,\n})\n\nfunction isLocked(c: CampaignView) {\n  return c.status !== 'draft' && c.status !== 'ready'\n}\n\nasync function load() {\n  loadError.value = null\n  try {\n    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)\n    if (isLocked(c)) {\n      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign-live', params: { id: c.id } })\n      return\n    }\n    campaign.value = c\n    drafts.start(c)\n  } catch (e) {\n    loadError.value =\n      e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404\n        ? 'not_found'\n        : isUnreachable(e)\n          ? 'unreachable'\n          : 'other'\n  }\n}\n\n/** After draft.done: the stored messages, their reports and the new status. */\nasync function refresh() {\n  try {\n    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)\n    campaign.value = c\n    if (!isLocked(c)) drafts.start(c)\n  } catch {\n    // The cards already hold the final results; the next load picks up the status.\n  }\n}\n\n/** The browser gave up on the stream: decide from the campaign whether to reopen it. */\nasync function checkAfterDisconnect(): Promise<'retry' | 'stop'> {\n  try {\n    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)\n    campaign.value = c\n    if (isLocked(c)) {\n      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign-live', params: { id: c.id } })\n      return 'stop'\n    }\n    if (!needsDrafting(c)) {\n      drafts.start(c) // everything is stored: show it, nothing left to stream\n      return 'stop'\n    }\n    return 'retry'\n  } catch (e) {\n    if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {\n      loadError.value = 'not_found'\n      return 'stop'\n    }\n    return 'retry'\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n\nconst channels = computed(() => campaign.value?.channels ?? [])\nconst allFinal = computed(() =>\n  channels.value.every((ch) => {\n    const d = drafts.progress.value.channels[ch]\n    return d !== undefined && isFinal(d.state)\n  }),\n)\n\nconst steps = computed<Step[]>(() => [\n  { label: 'Audience', status: 'done' },\n  { label: 'Messages', status: allFinal.value ? 'done' : 'current' },\n  { label: 'Review', status: allFinal.value ? 'current' : 'upcoming' },\n  { label: 'Send', status: 'upcoming' },\n])\n\nconst canReview = computed(\n  () =>\n    campaign.value !== null &&\n    campaign.value.status === 'ready' &&\n    allFinal.value &&\n    campaign.value.audience.channel_messages > 0,\n)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <section class=\"draft\">\n    <AppButton variant=\"ghost\" icon=\"arrow-left\" :to=\"{ name: 'composer' }\">New campaign</AppButton>\n\n    <template v-if=\"loadError\">\n

45 with \"Muhammed Emin\" (13) in place of the 14-character placeholder.\n    expect(c.get('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').text()).toBe('45 / 160')\n  })\n\n  it('says near limit and over limit in words', () => {\n    const near = card('sms', draft({ fields: { text: 'a'.repeat(144) } }))\n    expect(near.get('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').text()).toBe('Near limit144 / 160')\n    const over = card('sms', draft({ fields: { text: 'a'.repeat(150) + '{{first_name}}' } }))\n    expect(over.get('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').text()).toBe('3 over limit163 / 160')\n  })\n\n  it('shows the email subject with a counter and the body without one', () => {\n    const c = card(\n      'email',\n      draft({ state: 'valid', fields: { subject: '{{first_name}}, %30 indirim', body: 'Merhaba' } }),\n    )\n    expect(c.text()).toContain('Subject')\n    expect(c.text()).toContain('Body')\n    expect(c.findAll('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').map((x) => x.text())).toEqual(['27 / 80'])\n  })\n\n  it('keeps showing skeleton lines where the text is still streaming', () => {\n    const c = card('email', draft({ state: 'writing', fields: { subject: 'Konu', body: 'Merh' } }))\n    expect(c.findAll('.skeleton')).toHaveLength(1)\n    expect(c.findAll('.cursor')).toHaveLength(1)\n    const valid = card('email', draft({ state: 'valid', fields: { subject: 'Konu', body: 'Merhaba' } }))\n    expect(valid.findAll('.skeleton')).toHaveLength(0)\n    expect(valid.findAll('.cursor')).toHaveLength(0)\n  })\n\n  it('explains a manual edit next to the field and in the card', () => {\n    const report = {\n      valid: false,\n      errors: [{ field: 'text', code: 'too_long', message: 'text is 200 characters; the limit is 160.' }],\n      lengths: [],\n    } as unknown as Report\n    const c = card(\n      'sms',\n      draft({\n        state: 'needs_manual_edit',\n        attempt: 3,\n        fields: { text: 'x'.repeat(200) },\n        problems: ['text is 200 characters; the limit is 160.'],\n        report,\n      }),\n    )\n    expect(c.get('.field-errors').text()).toBe('text is 200 characters; the limit is 160.')\n

Bir hata yakaladım: `/campaigns/20`'den `/campaigns/21`'e geçince aynı bileşen yeniden kullanılıyor ve eski kampanya ekranda kalıyor. RouterView'a path'e göre `key` vererek düzeltiyorum.

<details>
<summary>Bash — Key the router view by path</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "src/App.vue"
s = open(p).read()
old = """      <main class="main">
        <RouterView />
      </main>"""
new = """      <main class="main">
        <!-- Keyed by path: /campaigns/20 → /campaigns/21 must load the other campaign,
             not keep the first one's state in a reused component. -->
        <RouterView :key="$route.path" />
      </main>"""
assert s.count(old) == 1
open(p, "w").write(s.replace(old, new))
EOF
npx vue-tsc --build && echo ok
```

```
ok
```

cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && cd frontend && npx prettier --check src/ >/dev/null && npx oxlint . 2>&1 | grep -cE ' error | warning ' ; cd .. && git add -A frontend && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
feat(frontend): draft screen

- /campaigns/:id (02): back link, title with the campaign status, the step
  indicator (Audience → Messages → Review → Send), the brief with "Edit
  brief", the audience and one card per selected channel
- audience card: conditions joined by "and"/"or", people, per channel the
  reachable count and the people left out for lack of consent, total
  messages (never called people), warning for criteria that could not be
  targeted, zero-audience and nobody-reachable states
- message card: Waiting, Writing… (pulse, cursor, skeleton), Validating…,
  Fixing… · attempt n of 3 (keeps the old text until the new attempt
  writes), Valid, Needs manual edit (problems and field errors); variables
  as pills; the counter shows the worst case with the audience's
  longest_values while the text streams
- useDraftStream: opens the stream only while a selected channel has no
  message, reloads the campaign on draft.done, and after a lost stream
  asks the campaign whether to reopen, show what is stored, or go to the
  dashboard
- the router view is keyed by path, so /campaigns/20 → /campaigns/21
  loads the other campaign (found in the browser: the first one stayed)

The counter shows the\n// worst case as the backend computes it (D15), the preview renders the\n// fictional sample user and the longest case. Save sends the edit through the\n// backend's validator (PUT …/messages/{channel}); a rejected edit leaves the\n// stored message unchanged and its errors appear next to the fields.\nimport { computed, nextTick, reactive, ref, useId } from 'vue'\nimport { saveMessage } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport { ApiError, isUnreachable } from '@/api/client'\nimport {\n  VARIABLES,\n  type Channel,\n  type Field,\n  type FieldError,\n  type MessageContent,\n  type Report,\n  type SaveMessageResponse,\n  type Values,\n  type Variable,\n} from '@/api/types'\nimport AppButton from '@/components/ui/AppButton.vue'\nimport AppChip from '@/components/ui/AppChip.vue'\nimport CharacterCounter from '@/components/ui/CharacterCounter.vue'\nimport {\n  fieldLimit,\n  fieldsOf,\n  insertAt,\n  placeholder,\n  renderMessage,\n  sampleUser,\n  worstCaseLength,\n  worstCaseValues,\n} from '@/logic/content'\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  campaignId: number\n  channel: Channel\n  initial: Partial<Record<Field, string>>\n  longest: Values\n}>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ saved: [SaveMessageResponse]; cancel: [] }>()\n\nconst uid = useId()\nconst LABELS: Record<Field, string> = { text: 'SMS text', subject: 'Subject', body: 'Body' }\n\nconst fields = fieldsOf(props.channel)\nconst values = reactive<Record<Field, string>>({\n  text: props.initial.text ?? '',\n  subject: props.initial.subject ?? '',\n  body: props.initial.body ?? '',\n})\nconst inputs = ref<Partial<Record<Field, HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>>>({})\n\nconst saving = ref(false)\nconst fieldErrors = ref<FieldError[]>([])\nconst failure = ref<string | null>(null)\n\n// The variable chips insert into the field that was focused last.\nconst target = ref<Field>(fields[0]!)\nconst selection = reactive({ start: 0, end: 0 })\n\nfunction remember(field: Field, e: Event) {\n  const el = e.target as HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement\n  target.value = field\n  selection.start = el.selectionStart ?? values[field].length\n  selection.end = el.selectionEnd ?? selection.start\n}\n\nasync function insert(v: Variable) {\n  const field = target.value\n  const { value, caret } = insertAt(values[field], selection.start, selection.end, placeholder(v))\n  values[field] = value\n  selection.start = selection.end = caret\n  await nextTick()\n  const el = inputs.value[field]\n  el?.focus()\n  el?.setSelectionRange(caret, caret)\n}\n\nfunction setInput(field: Field, el: unknown) {\n  if (el) inputs.value[field] = el as HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement\n}\n\nfunction content(): MessageContent {\n  return props.channel === 'sms'\n    ? { text: values.text }\n    : { subject: values.subject, body: values.body }\n}\n\nconst preview = computed(() => ({\n  sample: renderMessage(content(), sampleUser(props.longest)),\n  worst: renderMessage(content(), worstCaseValues(props.longest)),\n  sampleName: `${sampleUser(props.longest).first_name} ${sampleUser(props.longest).last_name}`,\n}))\n\nfunction errorsOf(field: Field) {\n  return fieldErrors.value.filter((e) => e.field === field)\n}\n\nfunction describedBy(field: Field) {\n  const ids = []\n  if (fieldLimit(field) > 0) ids.push(`${uid}-${field}-counter`)\n  if (errorsOf(field).length) ids.push(`${uid}-${field}-errors`)\n  return ids.join(' ') || undefined\n}\n\nasync function save() {\n  if (saving.value) return\n  saving.value = true\n  failure.value = null\n  try {\n    const res = await saveMessage(props.campaignId, props.channel, content())\n    fieldErrors.value = []\n    emit('saved', res)\n  } catch (e) {\n    const r

"""// Draft and review of one campaign (02 — Draft): the brief, the audience,
// and the messages streaming in; then editing, and the confirmation that
// starts sending. Approved campaigns open their dashboard.
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { approveCampaign, fetchCampaign } from '@/api/campaigns'
import { ApiError, isUnreachable } from '@/api/client'
import type { CampaignView, Channel, Report, SaveMessageResponse } from '@/api/types'
import AudienceCard from '@/components/campaign/AudienceCard.vue'
import ConfirmSendModal from '@/components/campaign/ConfirmSendModal.vue'
import MessageCard from '@/components/campaign/MessageCard.vue'
"""),
("""const steps = computed<Step[]>(() => [
  { label: 'Audience', status: 'done' },
  { label: 'Messages', status: allFinal.value ? 'done' : 'current' },
  { label: 'Review', status: allFinal.value ? 'current' : 'upcoming' },
  { label: 'Send', status: 'upcoming' },
])

const canReview = computed(
  () =>
    campaign.value !== null &&
    campaign.value.status === 'ready' &&
    allFinal.value &&
    campaign.value.audience.channel_messages > 0,
)

"""// ---- Editing ----

/** Channels with an edit in progress; their last validation no longer describes the text. */
const editing = reactive(new Set<Channel>())

function onEditing(channel: Channel, on: boolean) {
  if (on) editing.add(channel)
  else editing.delete(channel)
}

function onSaved(channel: Channel, res: SaveMessageResponse) {
  if (!campaign.value) return
  campaign.value = {
    ...campaign.value,
    status: res.status,
    messages: { ...campaign.value.messages, [channel]: res.message },
  }
  drafts.setMessage(channel, res.message)
}

// ---- Review & approve ----

const confirmOpen = ref(false)
const sending = ref(false)
const approveError = ref<{ title: string; details: string[] } | null>(null)

const steps = computed<Step[]>(() => [
  { label: 'Audience', status: 'done' },
  { label: 'Messages', status: allFinal.value ? 'done' : 'current' },
  {
    label: 'Review',
    status: confirmOpen.value ? 'done' : allFinal.value ? 'current' : 'upcoming',
  },
  { label: 'Send', status: confirmOpen.value ? 'current' : 'upcoming' },
])

const canReview = computed(
  () =>
    campaign.value !== null &&
    campaign.value.status === 'ready' &&
    allFinal.value &&
    editing.size === 0 &&
    campaign.value.audience.channel_messages > 0,
)

/** Why "Review & approve" is not available yet, in words. */
const reviewHint = computed(() => {
  const c = campaign.value
  if (!c || canReview.value) return ''
  if (!allFinal.value) return 'Available once both messages are written and checked.'
  if (editing.size > 0) return 'Save or cancel your edit first.'
  if (c.audience.channel_messages === 0) return 'Nobody in this audience can be reached.'
  return 'Every selected channel needs a valid message.'
})

function openConfirm() {
  approveError.value = null
  confirmOpen.value = true
}

async function approve() {
  if (sending.value || !campaign.value) return
  sending.value = true
  approveError.value = null
  try {
    const res = await approveCampaign(campaign.value.id)
    confirmOpen.value = false
    await router.push({ name: 'campaign-live', params: { id: res.campaign_id } })
  } cat

 "content": "import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'\nimport { flushPromises, mount } from '@vue/test-utils'\nimport type { Values } from '@/api/types'\nimport MessageEditor from '../MessageEditor.vue'\n\nconst longest: Values = {\n  first_name: 'Muhammed Emin',\n  last_name: 'Montgomery-Fitzgerald',\n  city: 'İstanbul',\n}\n\nfunction json(status: number, body: unknown): Response {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\n    status,\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n  })\n}\n\nlet fetchMock: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<typeof fetch>>\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  fetchMock = vi.fn<typeof fetch>()\n  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)\n})\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.unstubAllGlobals()\n  document.body.innerHTML = ''\n})\n\nfunction editor(channel: 'sms' | 'email', initial: Record<string, string>) {\n  return mount(MessageEditor, {\n    props: { campaignId: 5, channel, initial, longest },\n    attachTo: document.body,\n  })\n}\n\ndescribe('MessageEditor', () => {\n  it('inserts a variable at the caret and keeps typing there', async () => {\n    const e = editor('sms', { text: 'Merhaba , hoş geldin' })\n    const ta = e.get('textarea').element as HTMLTextAreaElement\n    ta.setSelectionRange(8, 8)\n    await e.get('textarea').trigger('select')\n\n    await e.get('button[aria-label=\"Insert first_name\"]').trigger('click')\n    expect(ta.value).toBe('Merhaba {{first_name}}, hoş geldin')\n    expect(ta.selectionStart).toBe(22)\n    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(ta)\n  })\n\n  it('updates the worst-case counter and the previews as you type', async () => {\n    const e = editor('sms', { text: '' })\n    await e.get('textarea').setValue('a'.repeat(147) + '{{first_name}}')\n    expect(e.get('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').text()).toBe('Near limit160 / 160')\n    await e.get('textarea').setValue('a'.repeat(148) + '{{first_name}}')\n    expect(e.get('[data-testid=\"counter\"]').text()).toBe('1 over limit161 / 160')\n\n    await e.get('textarea').setValue('Merhaba {{first_name}} {{city}}')\n    const previews = e.findAll('.preview-text').map((p) => p.text())\n    expect(previews).toEqual(['Merhaba Ayşe İstanbul', 'Merhaba Muhammed Emin İstanbul'])\n  })\n\n  it(\"saves through the backend's validator and reports the stored message\", async () => {\n    const saved = { message: { state: 'valid', source: 'edited' }, status: 'ready' }\n    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(json(200, saved))\n    const e = editor('email', { subject: 'Konu', body: 'Gövde' })\n    await e.get('input').setValue('{{first_name}}, sepetin seni bekliyor')\n    await e.get('form').trigger('submit')\n    await flushPromises()\n\n    const [url, init] = fetchMock.mock.calls[0]!\n    expect(url).toBe('/api/campaigns/5/messages/email')\n    expect(init?.method).toBe('PUT')\n    expect(JSON.parse(String(init?.body))).toEqual({\n      subject: '{{first_name}}, sepetin seni bekliyor',\n      body: 'Gövde',\n    })\n    expect(e.emitted('saved')).toEqual([[saved]])\n  })\n\n  it('shows a rejected edit next to its field and keeps the text', async () => {\n    const message =\n      'text is 163 characters with the longest values in this audience filled in; the limit is 160. Shorten it by at least 3 characters.'\n    fetchMock.mockResolvedValue(\n      json(422, {\n        error: 'invalid content',\n        report: { valid: false, errors: [{ field: 'text', code: 'too_long', message }] },\n      }),\n    )\n    const e = editor('sms', { text: 'a'.repeat(163) })\n    await e.get('form').trigger('submit')\n    await flushPromises()\n\n    const ta = e.get('textarea')\n    expect(ta.attributes('aria-invalid')).toBe('true')\n    const errorsId = ta.attributes('aria-describedby')!.split(' ').find((id) => id.endsWith('errors'))!\n    expect(document.getElementById(errorsId)!.textContent).toContain(me

object[]) => ({\n  valid: true,\n  errors: [],\n  lengths,\n  longest_values: {},\n  preview: { sample_user: {}, sample: {}, worst_case: {} },\n})\n\nconst sms: MessageView = {\n  state: 'valid',\n cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && cat > src/composables/useDeliveryStream.ts <<'EOF'
// The live dashboard's data: the snapshot stream of one campaign
// (GET /api/campaigns/{id}/events, docs/API.md). Each snapshot is the whole
// state, so it replaces the previous one; while the connection is lost the
// last snapshot stays on screen and the next one replaces it completely.
import { onBeforeUnmount, ref } from 'vue'
import { eventsURL } from '@/api/campaigns'
import type { DeliveryDoneEvent, Snapshot } from '@/api/types'
import { openStream, type ConnectionState, type StreamHandle } from '@/logic/stream'

/** Snapshots come every second; this much silence means the connection is gone. */
export const STALL_MS = 5000

export function useDeliveryStream(
  id: number,
  options: { check: () => Promise<'retry' | 'stop'> },
) {
  const snapshot = ref<Snapshot | null>(null)
  const connection = ref<ConnectionState>('connecting')
  /** The api could not read this second's snapshot (delivery.error); the shown one is older. */
  const stale = ref(false)
  const done = ref<DeliveryDoneEvent | null>(null)
  let handle: StreamHandle | null = null

  function start() {
    handle?.close()
    handle = openStream({
      url: eventsURL(id),
      handlers: {
        'delivery.snapshot': (d) => {
          snapshot.value = d as Snapshot
          stale.value = false
        },
        'delivery.error': () => {
          stale.value = true
        },
        'delivery.done': (d) => {
          done.value = d as DeliveryDoneEvent
        },
      },
      doneEvent: 'delivery.done',
      onState: (s) => (connection.value = s),
      check: options.check,
      stallMs: STALL_MS,
    })
  }

  function stop() {
    handle?.close()
    handle = null
  }

  onBeforeUnmount(stop)

  return { snapshot, connection, stale, done, start, stop, retryNow: () => handle?.retryNow() }
}
EOF
cat > src/components/ui/ProgressBar.vue <<'EOF'
<script setup lang="ts">
// A single progress bar. The fill uses the exact share; only the shown
// percentage is rounded (by the caller).
import { computed } from 'vue'

const props = defineProps<{ value: number; max: number; label: string }>()

const share = computed(() => (props.max > 0 ? Math.min(1, props.value / props.max) : 0))
</script>

<template>
  <div
    class="track"
    role="progressbar"
    :aria-valuenow="value"
    aria-valuemin="0"
    :aria-valuemax="max"
    :aria-valuetext="label"
    :aria-label="label"
  >
    <div class="fill" :style="{ width: `${share * 100}%` }"></div>
  </div>
</template>

<style scoped>
.track {
  height: 0.875rem;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--color-track);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.fill {
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  background: var(--color-campaign-sending);
  transition: width var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);
}
</style>

Our own SVG: the series\n// is replaced by each snapshot and drawn as is, so nothing is animated or\n// invented, and the chart simply stays still while the dashboard reconnects.\nimport { computed } from 'vue'\nimport type { Channel } from '@/api/types'\nimport { CHANNEL_LABELS } from '@/logic/labels'\nimport { monotonePath, type Point } from '@/logic/snapshot'\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  series: { channel: Channel; values: number[] }[]\n  limit: number\n  window: { from: string; to: string }\n  finished: boolean\n}>()\n\n// Drawing area inside the viewBox.\nconst W = 640\nconst H = 260\nconst LEFT = 44\nconst RIGHT = 620\nconst TOP = 24\nconst BOTTOM = 220\n\nconst yMax = computed(() => Math.max(props.limit, ...props.series.flatMap((s) => s.values), 1))\nconst y = (v: number) => BOTTOM - (v / yMax.value) * (BOTTOM - TOP)\nconst ticks = computed(() => {\n  const step = yMax.value <= 100 ? 20 : Math.ceil(yMax.value / 5 / 10) * 10\n  const out: number[] = []\n  for (let v = 0; v <= yMax.value; v += step) out.push(v)\n  return out\n})\n\nfunction points(values: number[]): Point[] {\n  const n = values.length\n  const dx = n > 1 ? (RIGHT - LEFT) / (n - 1) : 0\n  return values.map((v, i) => ({ x: LEFT + i * dx, y: y(v) }))\n}\n\nconst lines = computed(() =>\n  props.series.map((s) => {\n    const pts = points(s.values)\n    const line = monotonePath(pts)\n    const last = pts[pts.length - 1]\n    return {\n      channel: s.channel,\n      line,\n      area: pts.length > 1 ? `${line}L${RIGHT},${BOTTOM}L${LEFT},${BOTTOM}Z` : '',\n      last,\n    }\n  }),\n)\n\nconst clock = (iso: string) =>\n  new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' })\n\nconst xLabels = computed(() =>\n  props.finished\n    ? [\n        { x: LEFT, text: clock(props.window.from), anchor: 'start' },\n        { x: RIGHT, text: clock(props.window.to), anchor: 'end' },\n      ]\n    : [\n        { x: LEFT, text: '-30s', anchor: 'start' },\n        { x: LEFT + (RIGHT - LEFT) / 3, text: '-20s', anchor: 'middle' },\n        { x: LEFT + (2 * (RIGHT - LEFT)) / 3, text: '-10s', anchor: 'middle' },\n        { x: RIGHT, text: 'Now', anchor: 'end' },\n      ],\n)\n\n/** The chart in words, for screen readers (the per-channel numbers are also in the cards). */\nconst summary = computed(() => {\n  const parts = props.series.map((s) => {\n    const now = s.values[s.values.length - 1] ?? 0\n    const peak = Math.max(0, ...s.values)\n    return `${CHANNEL_LABELS[s.channel]} ${now} per second at the end, highest ${peak}`\n  })\n  return `Sending speed over 30 seconds. ${parts.join('; ')}. Limit ${props.limit} per second.`\n})\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <figure class=\"chart\">\n    <div class=\"legend\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n      <span v-for=\"s in series\" :key=\"s.channel\" class=\"legend-item\">\n        <span class=\"dot\" :style=\"{ background: `var(--color-${s.channel})` }\"></span>\n        {{ CHANNEL_LABELS[s.channel] }}\n      </span>\n    </div>\n    <svg :viewBox=\"`0 0 ${W} ${H}`\" role=\"img\" :aria-label=\"summary\" class=\"svg\">\n      <text :x=\"LEFT - 8\" :y=\"TOP - 10\" class=\"axis\" text-anchor=\"end\">msg/s</text>\n      <g v-for=\"t in ticks\" :key=\"t\">\n        <line :x1=\"LEFT\" :x2=\"RIGHT\" :y1=\"y(t)\" :y2=\"y(t)\" class=\"grid\" />\n        <text :x=\"LEFT - 8\" :y=\"y(t) + 4\" class=\"axis\" text-anchor=\"end\">{{ t }}</text>\n      </g>\n      <line :x1=\"LEFT\" :x2=\"RIGHT\" :y1=\"y(limit)\" :y2=\"y(limit)\" class=\"limit\" />\n      <text :x=\"RIGHT\" :y=\"y(limit) - 6\" class=\"axis limit-label\" text-anchor=\"end\">\n        Limit {{ limit }}/s\n      </text>\n      <g v-for=\"l in lines\" :key=\"l.ch

 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// Live dashboard of an approved campaign (08 — Live): overall progress, each\n// channel's states and rate, the speed chart and recent issues, from the\n// snapshot stream. A finished campaign shows its final state and keeps its chart.\nimport { computed, onMounted, ref, watch } from 'vue'\nimport { useRouter } from 'vue-router'\nimport { fetchCampaign } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport { ApiError, isUnreachable } from '@/api/client'\nimport type { CampaignView, Channel } from '@/api/types'\nimport ChannelProgress from '@/components/campaign/ChannelProgress.vue'\nimport RecentIssues from '@/components/campaign/RecentIssues.vue'\nimport SpeedChart from '@/components/campaign/SpeedChart.vue'\nimport AppBanner from '@/components/ui/AppBanner.vue'\nimport AppButton from '@/components/ui/AppButton.vue'\nimport GlassCard from '@/components/ui/GlassCard.vue'\nimport ProgressBar from '@/components/ui/ProgressBar.vue'\nimport SkeletonLines from '@/components/ui/SkeletonLines.vue'\nimport StatusPill from '@/components/ui/StatusPill.vue'\nimport { useDeliveryStream } from '@/composables/useDeliveryStream'\nimport { CAMPAIGN_STATUS_LABELS } from '@/logic/labels'\nimport { progressPercent } from '@/logic/snapshot'\n\nconst props = defineProps<{ id: number }>()\nconst router = useRouter()\n\nconst campaign = ref<CampaignView | null>(null)\nconst loadError = ref<'not_found' | 'unreachable' | null>(null)\n\nconst live = useDeliveryStream(props.id, { check: checkAfterDisconnect })\n\nasync function load() {\n  loadError.value = null\n  try {\n    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)\n    if (c.status === 'draft' || c.status === 'ready') {\n      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign', params: { id: c.id } })\n      return\n    }\n    campaign.value = c\n    live.start()\n  } catch (e) {\n    loadError.value = e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404 ? 'not_found' : 'unreachable'\n  }\n}\n\n/** The browser gave up on the stream (a 502 while the api restarts, or a 404/409). */\nasync function checkAfterDisconnect(): Promise<'retry' | 'stop'> {\n  try {\n    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)\n    if (c.status === 'draft' || c.status === 'ready') {\n      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign', params: { id: c.id } })\n      return 'stop'\n    }\n    return 'retry'\n  } catch (e) {\n    if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {\n      loadError.value = 'not_found'\n      return 'stop'\n    }\n    return 'retry' // still unreachable: keep the last snapshot and try again later\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n\nconst s = computed(() => live.snapshot.value)\nconst status = computed(() => s.value?.status ?? campaign.value?.status ?? <details>
<summary>Bash — Commit step S6</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && git add -A frontend && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
feat(frontend): live dashboard

- /campaigns/:id/live (08): title with the campaign status, "Started
  HH:MM:SS · N messages", Live / Reconnecting… / Final, overall progress
  ("860 of 1,226 done", never 100% early), one card per channel with its
  rate against the 100/s limit and Sent → Sending → Queued → Retrying →
  Failed (exact shares; the legend lists all five counts), the 30-second
  speed chart under the dashed limit, and recent issues worded in the UI
  and dated against the snapshot's own time
- useDeliveryStream: every snapshot replaces the last; delivery.done
  closes the stream; delivery.error marks the numbers as old; 5 s without
  a snapshot counts as a lost connection
- reconnecting keeps the last snapshot on screen with its time and a "Try
  now" button; after the browser gives up (502) the campaign is checked
  and the stream reopened with backoff; the next snapshot replaces the old
  one, so the numbers carry on and never reset
- a finished campaign keeps its chart ("last 30 seconds of sending", clock
  times on the axis); a draft or ready campaign is sent to its draft page
- SpeedChart: our own SVG with a monotone curve, an accessible summary,
  axis and legend; announcements only on status and connection changes

Checked live (mock LLM): weekend sale, 3,714 emails; `docker compose stop
api` at 826 done → Reconnecting… within 0.4 s, numbers kept; `start api`
→ live again at 2,458 after four 502s; completed at 3,714 (3,683 sent,
31 failed) and no request after delivery.done.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
git log --oneline -1
```

 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// A campaign in a list: name, channels, recipients, status, and progress\n// where it is known. The list endpoint has no delivery counts, so a sending\n// campaign shows an indeterminate bar rather than an invented percentage; a\n// finished one is 100% done (sent and failed are both final).\nimport { computed } from 'vue'\nimport type { CampaignSummary } from '@/api/types'\nimport StatusPill from '@/components/ui/StatusPill.vue'\nimport { CHANNEL_LABELS } from '@/logic/labels'\n\nconst props = defineProps<{ campaign: CampaignSummary }>()\n\nconst to = computed(() => ({\n  name:\n    props.campaign.status === 'draft' || props.campaign.status === 'ready'\n      ? 'campaign'\n      : 'campaign-live',\n  params: { id: props.campaign.id },\n}))\nconst finished = computed(\n  () => props.campaign.status === 'completed' || props.campaign.status === 'failed',\n)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <RouterLink :to=\"to\" class=\"card glass\" data-testid=\"campaign-card\">\n    <h3 class=\"name\">{{ campaign.name }}</h3>\n    <p class=\"channels caption\">\n      <span v-for=\"ch in campaign.channels\" :key=\"ch\" class=\"channel\">\n        <span class=\"dot\" :style=\"{ background: `var(--color-${ch})` }\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n        {{ CHANNEL_LABELS[ch] }}\n      </span>\n    </p>\n    <p class=\"recipients numeric\">\n      <strong>{{ campaign.audience.total.toLocaleString('en-US') }}</strong>\n      {{ campaign.audience.total === 1 ? 'recipient' : 'recipients' }}\n    </p>\n    <StatusPill kind=\"campaign\" :status=\"campaign.status\" />\n    <div v-if=\"finished\" class=\"progress\">\n      <span class=\"track\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><span class=\"fill\"></span></span>\n      <span class=\"caption secondary-text\">100% done</span>\n    </div>\n    <div v-else-if=\"campaign.status === 'sending'\" class=\"progress\">\n      <span class=\"track\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><span class=\"fill indeterminate\"></span></span>\n      <span class=\"caption secondary-text\">In progress</span>\n    </div>\n    <p v-else class=\"caption secondary-text\">Not sent yet</p>\n  </RouterLink>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.card {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  align-items: flex-start;\n  gap: var(--space-3);\n  padding: var(--space-5) var(--space-6);\n  text-decoration: none;\n  min-width: 0;\n  transition:\n    background-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui),\n    box-shadow var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);\n}\n\n.card:hover {\n  background: var(--color-glass-hover);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-raised), var(--shadow-highlight);\n}\n\n.name {\n  font-size: var(--radius-pill);\n}\n\n.progress {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: var(--space-3);\n  width: 100%;\n}\n\n.track {\n  position: relative;\n  flex: 1;\n  height: 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  background: var(--color-track);\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.fill {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);\n  background: {
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/views/CampaignsView.vue",
 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// All campaigns (GET /api/campaigns, the 50 most recent).\nimport { onMounted, ref } from 'vue'\nimport { fetchCampaigns } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport type { CampaignSummary } from '@/api/types'\nimport CampaignGrid from '@/components/campaign/CampaignGrid.vue'\nimport AppBanner from '@/components/ui/AppBanner.vue'\nimport AppButton from '@/components/ui/AppButton.vue'\nimport GlassCard from '@/components/ui/GlassCard.vue'\nimport SkeletonLines from '@/components/ui/SkeletonLines.vue'\n\nconst campaigns = ref<CampaignSummary[] | null>(null)\nconst failed = ref(false)\n\nasync function load() {\n  failed.value = false\n  try {\n    campaigns.value = await fetchCampaigns()\n  } catch {\n    failed.value = true\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <section class=\"campaigns\">\n    <h1>Campaigns</h1>\n    <AppBanner v-if=\"failed\" tone=\"error\" title=\"Can't reach the server\">\n      The campaigns could not be loaded.\n      <template #action>\n        <AppButton variant=\"secondary\" @click=\"load\">Try again</AppButton>\n      </template>\n    </AppBanner>\n    <GlassCard v-else-if=\"!campaigns\">\n      <p class=\"secondary-text\" role=\"status\">Loading campaigns…</p>\n      <SkeletonLines :lines=\"3\" />\n    </GlassCard>\n    <GlassCard v-else-if=\"campaigns.length === 0\">\n      <p>No campaigns yet.</p>\n      <div>\n        <AppButton variant=\"primary\" icon=\"plus\" :to=\"{ name: 'composer' }\">New campaign</AppButton>\n      </div>\n    </GlassCard>\n    <CampaignGrid v-else :campaigns=\"campaigns\" />\n  </section>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.campaigns {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: var(--layout-gap);\n}\n</style>\n"
}

```
{
 "file_path": "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend/src/views/LiveSendsView.vue",
 "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\n// \"Live sends\": opens the dashboard of the campaign that is sending now, or\n// of the latest approved one; says so when nothing has been sent yet.\nimport { onMounted, ref } from 'vue'\nimport { useRouter } from 'vue-router'\nimport { fetchCampaigns } from '@/api/campaigns'\nimport AppBanner from '@/components/ui/AppBanner.vue'\nimport AppButton from '@/components/ui/AppButton.vue'\nimport GlassCard from '@/components/ui/GlassCard.vue'\n\nconst router = useRouter()\nconst state = ref<'loading' | 'none' | 'failed'>('loading')\n\nasync function load() {\n  state.value = 'loading'\n  try {\n    const list = await fetchCampaigns() // newest first\n    const target =\n      list.find((c) => c.status === 'sending') ??\n      list.find((c) => c.status === 'completed' || c.status === 'failed')\n    if (target) {\n      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign-live', params: { id: target.id } })\n    } else {\n      state.value = 'none'\n    }\n  } catch {\n    state.value = 'failed'\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <section class=\"live-sends\">\n    <h1>Live sends</h1>\n    <AppBanner v-if=\"state === 'failed'\" tone=\"error\" title=\"Can't reach the server\">\n      The campaigns could not be loaded.\n      <template #action>\n        <AppButton variant=\"secondary\" @click=\"load\">Try again</AppButton>\n      </template>\n    </AppBanner>\n    <GlassCard v-else-if=\"state === 'none'\">\n      <p>Nothing has been sent yet. Approve a campaign to follow it here.</p>\n      <div>\n        <AppButton variant=\"primary\" icon=\"plus\" :to=\"{ name: 'composer' }\">New campaign</AppButton>\n      </div>\n    </GlassCard>\n    <p v-else class=\"secondary-text\" role=\"status\">Opening the latest send…</p>\n  </section>\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.live-sends {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: var(--layout-gap);\n}\n</style>\n"
}

p = "src/components/campaign/CampaignCard.vue"
s = open(p).read()
old = """// A campaign in a list: name, channels, people in the audience (not all of
// them may have consented to a channel), status, and progress where it is known. The list endpoint has no delivery counts, so a sending
// campaign shows an indeterminate bar rather than an invented percentage; a
// finished one is 100% done (sent and failed are both final)."""
new = """// A campaign in a list: name, channels, people in the audience (not all of
// them consented to every channel), status, and progress where it is known.
// The list endpoint has no delivery counts, so a sending campaign shows an
// indeterminate bar rather than an invented percentage; a finished one is
// 100% done (sent and failed are both final)."""
assert s.count(old) == 1
open(p, "w").write(s.replace(old, new))
EOF
npx prettier --write src/ >/dev/null; npx vue-tsc --build && npx eslint . && npx vitest --run 2>&1 | grep -E 'Tests |FAIL' && cd .. && git add -A frontend && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
feat(frontend): campaign list

The first UI piece to cut (PLAN.md cut order 3), so it is a separate step.

- home: "Recent campaigns", the four newest, with "All campaigns" when there
  are more; /campaigns lists the 50 the api returns
- card: name, channels, people in the audience, status; finished campaigns
  are 100% done, a sending one gets an indeterminate bar (the list has no
  delivery counts, so no percentage is invented), drafts say "Not sent yet";
  a card opens the draft or the dashboard by status
- "Live sends" opens the campaign sending now, else the latest finished one,
  else explains that nothing was sent yet
- sidebar: Campaigns and Live sends, active with aria-current

getComputedStyle(probe).color;\n  let m = c.match(/^rgba?\\(([^)]+)\\)/); if (m) { const p = m[1].split(/[ ,/]+/).filter(Boolean).map(Number); return [p[0]/255, p[1]/255, p[2]/255, p[3] ?? 1]; }\n  m = c.match(/^color\\(srgb ([^)]+)\\)/); if (m) { const p = m[1].split(/[ /]+/).filter(Boolean).map(Number); return [p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3] ?? 1]; }\n  throw new Error('unparsed ' + expr + ' -> ' + c); }\nconst over = (top, bottom) => { const a = top[3]; return [0,1,2].map(i => top[i]*a + bottom[i]*(1-a)).concat(1); };\nconst lum = c => { const f = v => v <= 0.04045 ? v/12.92 : ((v+0.055)/1.055)**2.4; return 0.2126*f(c[0]) + 0.7152*f(c[1]) + 0.0722*f(c[2]); };\nconst ratio = (fg, bg) => { const t = over(fg, bg); const a = lum(t), b = lum(bg); return (Math.max(a,b)+0.05)/(Math.min(a,b)+0.05); };\nconst bg = rgba('var(--color-bg)');\nconst bases = { page: bg };\nfor (const b of ['aqua','sky','peach']) { const c = rgba(`var(--color-blob-${b})`); c[3] = 0.7; bases['blob-' + b] = over(c, bg); }\nconst glass = rgba('var(--color-glass)'), field = rgba('var(--color-field)'), hover = rgba('var(--color-glass-hover)');\nconst stack = (layers, base) => layers.reduce((acc, l) => over(rgba(l), acc), base);\nconst cases = [\n  ['text on glass', 'var(--color-text)', ['var(--color-glass)']],\n  ['secondary on glass', 'var(--color-text-secondary)', ['var(--color-glass)']],\n  ['secondary on field', 'var(--color-text-secondary)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-field)']],\n  ['secondary on page', 'var(--color-text-secondary)', []],\n  ['white on AA gradient start', 'var(--color-white)', ['var(--color-primary-aa-start)']],\n  ['white on AA gradient end', 'var(--color-white)', ['var(--color-primary-aa-end)']],\n  ['step current (aa-start) on page', 'var(--color-primary-aa-start)', []],\n  ['Live (completed-ink) on page', 'var(--color-campaign-completed-ink)', []],\n  ['near limit (retrying-ink) on field', 'var(--color-message-retrying-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-field)']],\n  ['over limit (failed-ink) on field', 'var(--color-campaign-failed-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-field)']],\n  ['field error (failed-ink) on glass', 'var(--color-campaign-failed-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)']],\n  ['dialog text-secondary on glass-hover', 'var(--color-text-secondary)', ['var(--color-glass-hover)']],\n];\nfor (const f of ['sms','email','campaign-draft','campaign-ready','campaign-sending','campaign-completed','campaign-failed','message-queued','message-sending','message-sent','message-retrying','message-failed']) {\n  cases.push([`pill ${f} ink on tint over glass`, `var(--color-${f}-ink)`, ['var(--color-glass)', `var(--color-${f}-tint)`]]);\n}\nfor (const f of ['message-retrying','campaign-sending','campaign-failed']) cases.push([`banner ${f} ink on tint over page`, `var(--color-${f}-ink)`, [`var(--color-${f}-tint)`]]);\nfor (const f of ['sms','email']) cases.push([`variable token ${f} on tint over field`, `var(--color-${f}-ink)`, ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-field)', `var(--color-${f}-tint)`]]);\nconst rows = cases.map(([name, fg, layers]) => { const rs = Object.entries(bases).map(([k, b]) => [k, ratio(rgba(fg), stack(layers, b))]); const worst = rs.reduce((a, b) => a[1] < b[1] ? a : b); return `${worst[1] >= 4.5 ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${worst[1].toFixed(2)}:1  ${name} (worst over ${worst[0]})`; });\nprobe.remove(); rows.join('\\n')\n"
}

edits = {

"""  --color-border-control: var(--color-text-secondary);
  /* Supporting text: the secondary color reaches 4.5:1 on glass but not directly
     on the page over the sky glow (3.99:1, measured), so text outside a card uses this. */
  --color-text-supporting: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-text-secondary) 75%, var(--color-text));"""),
],
"src/assets/base.css": [
(""".secondary-text {
  color: var(--color-text-secondary);
}""",

  color: var(--color-text-supporting);
}"""),
],
"src/components/ui/StepIndicator.vue": [
(""".label {
  font-size: var(--font-size-body);
  color: var(--color-text-secondary);""",
""".label {
  font-size: var(--font-size-body);
  color: var(--color-text-supporting);"""),
(""".current .label {
  color: var(--color-primary-aa-start);
  font-weight: 600;
}""",
"""/* The coral marker is the accent; the label stays dark enough to read (3.6:1 in coral). */
.current .label {
  color: var(--color-text);
  font-weight: 600;
}"""),
],
"src/components/ui/AppBanner.vue": [
("""  border: var(--border-thin) solid var(--banner-border);
  background: var(--banner-tint);""",

  /* The tint over a glass base, so the ink keeps its contrast over the glows too. */
  background-color: var(--color-glass);
  background-image: linear-gradient(var(--banner-tint), var(--banner-tint));"""),
],
"src/components/ui/AppButton.vue": [

"""/* Primary: the AA gradient keeps white text at 4.5:1; the gloss stays in the top
   quarter, above the text, so it never lightens the background behind the label. */"""),
("""  inset: 0 0 60% 0;""", """  inset: 0 0 75% 0;"""),

v/12.92 : ((v+0.055)/1.055)**2.4; return 0.2126*f(c[0]) + 0.7152*f(c[1]) + 0.0722*f(c[2]); };\nconst ratio = (fg, bg) => { const t = over(fg, bg); const a = lum(t), b = lum(bg); return (Math.max(a,b)+0.05)/(Math.min(a,b)+0.05); };\nconst bg = rgba('var(--color-bg)'); const bases = { page: bg };\nfor (const b of ['aqua','sky','peach']) { const c = rgba(`var(--color-blob-${b})`); c[3] = 0.7; bases['blob-' + b] = over(c, bg); }\nconst stack = (layers, base) => layers.reduce((acc, l) => over(rgba(l), acc), base);\nconst cases = [\n  ['supporting text on page', 'var(--color-text-supporting)', []],\n  ['supporting text on glass', 'var(--color-text-supporting)', ['var(--color-glass)']],\n  ['step current label (text) on page', 'var(--color-text)', []],\n  ['banner retrying ink on tint over glass', 'var(--color-message-retrying-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-message-retrying-tint)']],\n  ['banner sending ink on tint over glass', 'var(--color-campaign-sending-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-campaign-sending-tint)']],\n  ['banner failed ink on tint over glass', 'var(--color-campaign-failed-ink)', ['var(--color-glass)', 'var(--color-campaign-failed-tint)']],\n];\nconst out = cases.map(([name, fg, layers]) => { const rs = Object.entries(bases).map(([k, b]) => [k, ratio(rgba(fg), stack(layers, b))]); const w = rs.reduce((a, b) => a[1] < b[1] ? a : b); return `${w[1] >= 4.5 ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${w[1].toFixed(2)}:1  ${name} (worst over ${w[0]})`; });\nconst btn = document.querySelector('.btn-primary'); const label = btn?.querySelector('span:last-child'); const g = btn && getComputedStyle(btn, '::before');\nout.push(`gloss bottom edge ${btn ? (btn.getBoundingClientRect().height * 0.25).toFixed(1) : '?'}px, label top ${btn && label ? (label.getBoundingClientRect().top - btn.getBoundingClientRect().top).toFixed(1) : '?'}px`);\nprobe.remove(); out.join('\\n')\n"
}
```

<summary>Bash — Size the sidebar in rem so it grows with text</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "src/assets/theme.css"
s = open(p).read()
old = """  --sidebar-width: 256px;
  --sidebar-compact-width: 224px;"""
new = """  /* In rem (256px and 224px by default) so the navigation grows with the text size. */
  --sidebar-width: 16rem;
  --sidebar-compact-width: 14rem;"""
assert s.count(old) == 1
open(p, "w").write(s.replace(old, new))
EOF
echo ok

<details>
<summary>Bash — Commit the accessibility fixes</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && npx prettier --check src/ >/dev/null && npx vue-tsc --build && npx eslint . && npx vitest --run 2>&1 | grep -E 'Tests |FAIL' && cd .. && git add -A frontend && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
fix(frontend): contrast and text size

Measured in the browser: every text color composited over its glass layers
and over the worst glow (sky at 70%), against WCAG AA 4.5:1.

- secondary text placed directly on the page measured 3.99:1; a derived
  --color-text-supporting (75% secondary, 25% text) gives 5.48:1 and is
  used by .secondary-text, the step labels and the connection indicator
- the current step's label was coral (3.61:1); the design asks for a
  darker readable label next to the coral marker, so it is now text color
- banners compose their tint over glass (the amber warning ink measured
  4.20:1 on a bare tint over the page, 4.92:1 over glass)
- the primary button's gloss now ends where the label begins (12 px of
  48), so it never lightens the background behind the text
- the sidebar is sized in rem (same 256/224 px by default): at 200% text
  its items were cut off ("Compo", "Campai"); now nothing is clipped and
  the page has no horizontal overflow at 1024 px

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
git log --oneline -1
```

async function load() {
  loadError.value = null
  try {
    const c = await fetchCampaign(props.id)
    if (c.status === 'draft' || c.status === 'ready') {
      await router.replace({ name: 'campaign', params: { id: c.id } })
      return
    }
    campaign.value = c
    live.start()
  } catch (e) {
    loadError.value = e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404 ? 'not_found' : 'unreachable'
```

<summary>Bash — Transition nav text color with its background</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "src/components/layout/AppSidebar.vue"
s = open(p).read()
old = "  transition: background-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);\n}\n\n.nav-item:hover {"
assert s.count(old) == 1, s
s = s.replace(old, "  /* Text and background change together; changing only the background left the\n     label unreadable for a moment when the active item moved (dark on navy). */\n  transition:\n    background-color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui),\n    color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-ui);\n}\n\n.nav-item:hover {")
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF
npx prettier --check src/ >/dev/null && npx vue-tsc --build && npx vitest --run 2>&1 | grep -E 'Tests |FAIL'
```

Tüm adımlar commit'lendi. Şimdi S9 dokümanları: PLAN.md'ye P6 doğrulama kaydı, DECISIONS.md'de D17'nin kapatılması ve yeni D26.

<details>
<summary>Bash — Close D17 and add D26 to DECISIONS.md</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "DECISIONS.md"
s = open(p).read()
reps = [
("| D17 | Frontend stack | Accepted (UI library to revisit in P6, see D22) |",
 "| D17 | Frontend stack | Accepted (P6: own components, no UI library, D26) |"),
("| D25 | Tracking as built (P5) | Accepted (P5) |",
 "| D25 | Tracking as built (P5) | Accepted (P5) |\n| D26 | Frontend as built (P6) | Accepted (P6) |"),
("""- **Component library:** Naive UI or PrimeVue, decided at the start of P6. To revisit: the
  design's glass style (D22) may be easier with our own small components than with a themed library.""",
"""- **Component library:** none *(decided in P6, D26 K1)*. The plan left Naive UI or PrimeVue open;
  the glass style (D22) is a set of tokens plus about fifteen small components, and the two parts that
  are hard to get right (a modal dialog and a choice of tone) are native elements (`<dialog>`,
  `<select>`). No state library and no chart library either (D26 K2, K3)."""),
]
for a, b in reps:
    assert s.count(a) == 1, a[:60]
    s = s.replace(a, b)

d26 = """
## D26 — Frontend as built (P6)

Decisions taken at the start of P6 (K1–K11, approved together) and while building it. Evidence: PLAN.md →
Verification log → P6. The design stays a visual reference (D22); where it and these decisions differ, the
difference is named below.

| # | Topic | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| K1 | UI library | Own small components (`AppButton`, `GlassCard`, `StatusPill`, `AppDialog`, …) on native elements; no UI library (closes D17) | The glass look is tokens and a few shapes; a themed library would have to be overridden piece by piece. Native `<dialog>` gives an inert background, Escape and a top layer. Every line can be explained in the review |
| K2 | Chart | Own SVG: the 30-value series per channel, fixed axis up to the limit, dashed 100/s line, an accessible text summary. The curve is a monotone cubic (Steffen tangents) | 2 × 30 points need no library. An ordinary spline bulges above a run of 95–100/s and would draw the line over the limit the chart is there to prove; with Steffen tangents every control point stays between its two samples (tested) |
| K3 | State | Composables (`useDraftStream`, `useDeliveryStream`, `useConfig`) over plain TypeScript in `src/logic/` (reducer, counter, snapshot wording, stream); no Pinia | Screens share only `/api/config`; each screen reloads its campaign by id, so a page reload works the same. The logic is tested without Vue |
| K4 | Server-sent events | `EventSource` plus our own reconnect: the browser's retry when an open stream ends; after a non-200 answer (the 502 while the api restarts) the browser gives up, so we wait 1, 2, 4, 8, 10 s (±20%), ask the campaign (`GET`) whether to reopen, stop or change screen, and open a new stream. The dashboard also treats 5 s without a snapshot as a lost connection. `es.close()` on `draft.done` / `delivery.done` | `EventSource` hides the status code of a failed open; the follow-up `GET` tells a restart (retry) from a 404 or an unapproved campaign (stop). A `fetch` stream reader would see the code but needs our own SSE parser, and the contract was written for `EventSource` (`retry: 3000`) |
| K5 | Counter | The backend's parser, renderer and worst-case length ported to `src/logic/content.ts` (code points, invalid variables counted as written); the backend stays the judge on save. **API addition:** the campaign view's `audience.longest_values` per channel (`content.WorstCaseValues`) | The counter must show the number the validator enforces (D15) while a message streams and for a channel the model never wrote (declined twice, timed out), when no report exists yet. A test compares the port with 720 answers recorded from the live api (`scripts/record-worst-case.mjs`) |
| K6 | Fonts | `@fontsource-variable/inter`, `/urbanist`, `/jetbrains-mono` 5.3.0 (OFL), imported in `main.ts` | Self-hosted, no request to Google Fonts; each subs
… [2889 more characters truncated]
```

 1 file changed, 40 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)

<details>
<summary>Bash — Record the P6 verification log in PLAN.md</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "docs/PLAN.md"
s = open(p).read()
old = "| P6 | Frontend | ⏳ next | — |"
assert s.count(old) == 1
s = s.replace(old, "| P6 | Frontend | ✅ done, in review (branch `feat/p6-frontend`) | 2026-10-21 |")
old = "| P7 | Hardening & submission | ⬜ | — |"
assert s.count(old) == 1
s = s.replace(old, "| P7 | Hardening & submission | ⏳ next | — |")

log = r"""
### P6

Everything below ran against the live stack with the mock LLM (`LLM_PROVIDER=mock`, `/api/config` checked before
creating campaigns), first through Vite's dev server and at the end through the production build behind nginx
(`:8080`). No paid call was made.

**Done criteria:**

| Criterion | Evidence | Result |
|---|---|---|
| The whole flow works in the browser with the mock LLM | production build: Brief library → "Cart reminder — İstanbul" → Generate → draft → Review & approve → Send messages → dashboard | SMS valid after ~1.0 s while the email was still being written, email valid at ~1.8 s, status Ready; approval opened the dashboard, which ended Completed with 213 of 213 done (SMS 89 sent, 1 failed; email 123 sent) and its chart and issues kept |
| An over-limit edit is refused by the backend and shown in the UI | `[test] Every SMS answer is 200 characters long…` → SMS "Needs manual edit" → Edit → Save as it was | `422`; next to the field: "text is 201 characters with the longest values in this audience filled in ({{first_name}} = "Muhammed Emin", 13 characters); the limit is 160. Shorten it by at least 41 characters."; the counter showed "41 over limit 201 / 160" before saving. A shortened text with `{{first_name}}` inserted at the caret saved as Valid (79 / 160) and the campaign became Ready |
| The dashboard follows the stream, shows Reconnecting… during `docker compose stop api` / `start api`, and carries on without resetting | weekend sale, 3,714 emails; the page's numbers sampled every 200 ms | live 150 → 826 done; Reconnecting… in the first sample after the stop, banner "Showing the last update from 15:03:28", numbers kept at 826; after `start api` four `502`s (backoff), then live again at 2,458; never lower than before; Completed at 3,714 (3,683 sent, 31 failed). No request after `delivery.done` |
| The counter equals the backend's `worst_case_length` (test) | `content.spec.ts`: the port against 720 answers recorded from the live api by `scripts/record-worst-case.mjs` | equal for all 720 (broken variables, Turkish letters, combining marks, characters outside the BMP, SMS text and email subject, two audiences with three sets of longest values). Live: SMS 109 / 160 and subject 46 / 80 on screen = the report's 109 and 46; `audience.longest_values` = each report's `longest_values` |
| Vitest tests for the draft stream and snapshot logic, plus component tests; `make test` green | `make test` | exit 0: 16 Go packages (`-race`, Postgres); frontend `vue-tsc --build`, then 12 files / 108 tests |
| 1440 and 1024 px layouts, keyboard and focus, reduced motion | in-app browser | see below; reduced motion could not be emulated there |

**The draft states on screen** (VIP thanks — İzmir, the SMS repaired on attempt 2, recorded with a `MutationObserver`):
SMS Writing… with the counter growing, "Near limit" at 146, "18 over limit" at 178, Validating…, "Fixing… · attempt 2
of 3" with the old text kept, the new attempt's text replacing it from 3 / 160, Validating…, Valid at 109 / 160; the email
became Valid while the SMS was being fixed. `[test] The SMS is declined twice…`: "Needs manual edit" with the refusal's
category, "No text yet.", Review & approve disabled with the reason in words.

**Layout and accessibility (in-app browser):**
- 1440 and 1024 px: composer, draft, confirm dialog, dashboard, campaign list; at 1024 the draft columns, the channel
  cards and chart/issues stack; no horizontal overflow (`scrollWidth = clientWidth`) on any screen.
- 200% text (root font size doubled) at 1024 px
… [6174 more characters truncated]

<details>
<summary>Bash — Make the dialog surface opaque</summary>

```
cd "~/Desktop/Smart Campagin Composer Case/frontend" && python3 -I - <<'EOF'
p = "src/components/ui/AppDialog.vue"
s = open(p).read()
old = """  color: var(--color-text);
  background: var(--color-glass-hover);"""
new = """  color: var(--color-text);
  /* Opaque: a dialog sits over text, and through 72% white the cards behind it
     stayed readable and competed with its content (seen where backdrop blur
     was not applied). Border, radius and shadow keep the glass look. */
  background: var(--color-glass-opaque);"""
assert s.count(old) == 1
open(p, "w").write(s.replace(old, new))
EOF
npx prettier --check src/ >/dev/null && npx vue-tsc --build && npx vitest --run 2>&1 | grep -E 'Tests |FAIL'
